📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期禽流感病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 病变和病毒抗原分布因物种而异,鹤显示出广泛的器官感染,包括中枢神经系统(CNS)、胰腺、脾脏、肝脏和肾脏,而其他物种则表现出更有针对性的器官受累。系统发育分析确定了 EA-2024-DI.2.1 亚系,与鹤形成单系簇。
  • 2.5% 的测试动物中检测到 H9N2 抗体,而 2.9%–4.5% 的动物中检测到 H5N1 抗体。所有 H9N2 阳性标本均来自同一省的 3 个地点 (p = 0.005),H5N1 抗体检出率因省而异(0%、1.3% 和 4.8%;p = 0.049)。
  • 该研究发现了一个涉及 Mareca penelope 和多种滨鸟(Tringa、Himantopus)的新型 H14 传播 网络,表明存在多宿主传播机制。三种分离株(SZ3742/H14N6、ZJ3523/H14N7 和 SZ3536/H14N8)表现出双重受体结合特异性,可识别鸟类型(α-2,3)和人类型(α-2,6)唾液酸。所有三种测试分离株均在小鼠肺部以低滴度短暂复制,病毒致病性有限。
  • 该研究确定 HA E185K 突变 是一种新型免疫逃逸 突变,可增强与禽类和人型唾液酸受体的结合。然而,雪貂研究表明,该 突变 并未将 传播 赋予直接或间接接触的雪貂。 [10.21203/rs.3.rs-11578744/v1]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Lesions and viral antigen distribution varied by species, with cranes showing infection in a broad organ spectrum, including the central nervous system (CNS), pancreas, spleen, liver, and kidneys, whereas other species exhibited a more targeted organ involvement.
  • H9N2 antibodies were detected in 2.5% of tested animals, while H5N1 antibodies were found in 2.9%–4.5%.
  • The study identified a novel H14 transmission network involving Mareca penelope and multiple shorebird species (Tringa, Himantopus), suggesting a multihost dispersal mechanism. [10.1080/22221751.2026.2758537].
  • The study identified the HA E185K mutation as a novel immune escape mutation that enhances binding to avian- and human-type sialic acid receptors.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 华盛顿州亚基马县一名居民因接触受感染奶牛而死于禽流感,这是美国2026年的首例死亡病例。
  • 亚基马县居民因禽流感并发症死亡,且有基础健康问题。
  • 该病例是自2024年2月以来美国第三例禽流感死亡病例。
  • 疾病预防控制中心确认病毒为H5N1,但未提及人传人传播情况。
  • 卫生官员正在追踪与死者有密切接触的人员。
  • A resident of Yakima County, Washington, has died from avian influenza after exposure to infected dairy cattle, marking the first U.S. death of the year.
  • The individual had underlying health conditions and died from complications of avian influenza.
  • This is the third confirmed human case of avian influenza in the U.S. since February 2024.
  • The CDC confirmed the virus as H5N1, but no evidence of human-to-human transmission was reported.
  • Health officials are conducting contact tracing to monitor potential exposure.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: bioRxiv (Cold Spring Harbor Laboratory)  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-31  |  DOI: 10.64898/2026.10.28.762465  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

奶牛运输网络控制着 H5N1 B3.13 在美国的传播和持续性

Dairy cattle movement networks govern the spread and persistence of H5N1 B3.13 in the United States
作者： Jonathan E. Pekar, Yu-Cai Shao, Alvin Crespo-Bellido, Prathyush Sambaturu, Guy Baele, Joel O. Wertheim, Praneeth Gangavarapu, Karthik Gangavarapu, Gytis Dudas, Thomas P. Peacock
摘要中文翻译摘要 禽流感 病毒 H5N1 主要存在于野生和家养鸟类中。H5N1 基因型 B3.13 于 2024 年 3 月首次在美国奶牛中检测到,并于 2024 年和 2025 年在该国大部分地区传播,形成家养哺乳动物中已知最大的 H5N1 暴发。通过分析病毒基因组数据和历史牛运动模式,我们发现活牛运动是 H5N1 空间传播的主要贡献者,而不是野生或家养鸟类。使用系统发育地理学方法,我们估计超过 40% 的州际病毒 传播 发生在 B3.13 首次检测之前,超过 70% 发生在 2024 年 4 月实施州际奶牛流动限制之前。实施后,州际 传播 放缓,但州内传播持续存在,受当地牛网络密度和流动量的推动。我们的流行病模拟表明,德克萨斯州狭长地带(记录了最早的病例)内部和周边县的连通性使该地区容易成为 暴发 震中。反事实模拟表明,考虑到检测前隐秘传播的程度,州际限制需要提前 2-3 个月开始,才能实质性地限制动物流行。网络通知的干预措施优于地理定义的遏制措施,但响应速度(而不是干预强度)是我们的流行病模型中的限制因素,这强调了 监测 系统能够在广泛传播之前检测病原体出现的需要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解控制 H5N1 B3.13 在美国传播和持续存在的因素,解决在家养哺乳动物中确定其 暴发 的主要驱动因素的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究分析了病毒基因组数据以及美国历史牛的流动模式,重点关注 2024 年 3 月至 2025 年 H5N1 B3.13 在奶牛中的传播。
核心方法
使用系统地理学方法来估计病毒 传播 模式,将基因组数据与牛运动网络相结合,以评估活牛运动在空间传播中的作用。
主要结果
分析显示,超过 40% 的州际病毒 传播 发生在 B3.13 首次检测之前,超过 70% 发生在 2024 年 4 月实施州际流动限制之前。
解释与创新
研究结果强调,活牛移动是 H5N1 空间传播的主要驱动力,挑战了 野生鸟类 是其传播的主要贡献者的假设。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了监测牛群移动网络对于及早发现 H5N1 暴发 的重要性,并表明基于这些网络的有针对性的干预措施可以改善遏制工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于历史运动数据和基因组序列,可能无法捕获所有 传播 动态,并且由于依赖间接数据源,证据强度中等。

Dairy cattle movement networks govern the spread and persistence of H5N1 B3.13 in the United States

Authors: Jonathan E. Pekar, Yu-Cai Shao, Alvin Crespo-Bellido, Prathyush Sambaturu, Guy Baele, Joel O. Wertheim, Praneeth Gangavarapu, Karthik Gangavarapu, Gytis Dudas, Thomas P. Peacock
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Avian influenza virus H5N1 is predominantly found in wild and domestic birds. H5N1 genotype B3.13 was first detected in United States dairy cattle in March 2024 and spread through much of the country during 2024 and 2025, resulting in the largest known H5N1 outbreak in domestic mammals. By analysing viral genomic data jointly with historical cattle movement patterns, we find that live cattle movement is the predominant contributor to H5N1 spatial spread, rather than wild or domestic birds. Using phylogeographic methods we estimate that >40% of interstate viral transmissions occurred before B3.13’s first detection and >70% occurred before interstate dairy cattle movement restrictions were implemented in April 2024. After this implementation, interstate transmission slowed but within-state spread persisted, driven by local cattle network density and movement volume. Our epidemic simulations demonstrate that the connectivity of counties within and around the Texas Panhandle—where the earliest cases were documented—predisposed the region to become an outbreak epicentre. Counterfactual simulations indicate that interstate restrictions needed to have begun 2–3 months earlier in order to limit the epizootic substantively, given the extent of cryptic spread before detection. Network-informed interventions outperformed geographically-defined containment, but response speed (rather than intervention strength) was the limiting factor in our epidemic models, underscoring the need for surveillance systems capable of detecting pathogen emergence before widespread dissemination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the factors governing the spread and persistence of H5N1 B3.13 in the United States, addressing the challenge of identifying the primary drivers of its outbreak in domestic mammals.
Design and population
The study analyzed viral genomic data alongside historical cattle movement patterns in the United States, focusing on the spread of H5N1 B3.13 in dairy cattle from March 2024 through 2025.
Core methods
Phylogeographic methods were used to estimate viral transmission patterns, integrating genomic data with cattle movement networks to assess the role of live cattle movement in spatial spread.
Main results
The analysis revealed that >40% of interstate viral transmissions occurred before B3.13 was first detected, and >70% occurred prior to the implementation of interstate movement restrictions in April 2024.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight that live cattle movement is the main driver of H5N1 spatial spread, challenging the assumption that wild birds are the primary contributors to its dissemination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results emphasize the importance of monitoring cattle movement networks for early detection of H5N1 outbreaks and suggest that targeted interventions based on these networks could improve containment efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on historical movement data and genomic sequences, which may not capture all transmission dynamics, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on indirect data sources.

进化枝 2.3.4.4b A(H5Nx) 高致病性 禽流感 病毒新出现的血凝素变体造成的人畜共患风险增加

Increased Zoonotic Risk Posed by Emerging Haemagglutinin Variants of Clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5Nx) High Pathogenicity Avian Influenza Virus
作者： Munir Iqbal, Jiayun Yang, Elizabeth Billington, William T. Harvey, Sofia Riccio, Simon Johnson, Valdez, Hannah Klim, Ksenia Sukhova, Jin Yu
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性 禽流感 病毒(HPAIV)仍然是对动物和人类健康的主要威胁。2.3.4.4b H5Nx 泛动物疾病分支的特点是跨物种适应能力增强,包括奶牛中的 暴发。野生和圈养种群中哺乳动物感染的激增加剧了人们对人畜共患和大流行潜力的担忧。在这里,我们分析了 2020 年至 2025 年进化枝 2.3.4.4b H5Nx 病毒中的血凝素 (HA) 进化,重点关注受体结合域内的关键残基。我们将 HA E185K(成熟 H5 HA 编号;H3 编号中的 E189K)确定为一种新型免疫逃逸 突变,可增强与禽类和人型唾液酸受体的结合。引人注目的是,这种 突变 在牛源性 H5N1 病毒中越来越普遍,并增强了与人类样受体的结合。然而,雪貂研究表明,虽然 E185K 病毒的致病性与牛源分离株相当,但 E185K 突变 并未将 传播 赋予直接或间接接触的雪貂。我们的结果揭示了一种将免疫逃逸与扩大的受体特异性联系起来的机制,突出了哺乳动物适应的潜在途径。这些发现强调需要持续不断进化的 H5Nx 病毒来减轻新出现的人畜共患病威胁。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决进化枝 2.3.4.4b A(H5Nx) 高致病性 禽流感 病毒的新出现的血凝素 (HA) 变体所带来的日益增加的人畜共患风险,这些病毒已表现出跨物种适应性,包括奶牛中的 暴发。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2020 年至 2025 年 2.3.4.4b H5Nx 病毒分支中的 HA 进化,重点关注来自包括牛和雪貂在内的各种 宿主 样本中受体结合域内的关键残基。
核心方法
研究人员使用序列分析来识别免疫逃逸 突变,特别是 HA E185K 突变,并进行雪貂研究以评估致病性和 传播 潜力。
主要结果
该研究确定 HA E185K 突变 是一种新型免疫逃逸 突变,可增强与禽类和人型唾液酸受体的结合。然而,雪貂研究表明,该 突变 并未将 传播 赋予直接或间接接触的雪貂。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调了哺乳动物适应的潜在途径,将免疫逃逸与扩大的受体特异性联系起来。这种 突变 在牛源性 H5N1 病毒中越来越普遍,并增强与人类样受体的结合。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要持续不断进化的 H5Nx 病毒 监测 来减轻新出现的人畜共患威胁,特别是考虑到免疫逃逸和受体特异性扩展的潜力。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用雪貂模型,该模型可能无法完全代表人类 传播 动力学。由于使用了动物模型并且需要进一步的人体研究,证据强度中等。

Increased Zoonotic Risk Posed by Emerging Haemagglutinin Variants of Clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5Nx) High Pathogenicity Avian Influenza Virus

Authors: Munir Iqbal, Jiayun Yang, Elizabeth Billington, William T. Harvey, Sofia Riccio, Simon Johnson, Valdez, Hannah Klim, Ksenia Sukhova, Jin Yu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract High pathogenicity avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) remain a major threat to animal and human health. The clade 2.3.4.4b H5Nx panzootic has been marked by increasing cross-species adaptation, including outbreaks in dairy cattle. The surge in mammalian infections in both wild and captive populations heighten concerns about zoonotic and pandemic potential. Here, we analyse haemagglutinin (HA) evolution in clade 2.3.4.4b H5Nx viruses from 2020 to 2025, focusing on key residues within the receptor-binding domain. We identify HA E185K (mature H5 HA numbering; E189K in H3 numbering) as a novel immune escape mutation that confers increased binding to avian- and human-type sialic acid receptors. Strikingly, this mutation is increasingly prevalent in cattle-derived H5N1 viruses and enhances binding to human-like receptors. However, ferret studies demonstrated that whilst pathogenicity of the E185K virus was comparable to the cattle derived isolate, the E185K mutation did not confer transmission to direct or indirect contact ferrets. Our results reveal a mechanism linking immune escape with expanded receptor specificity, highlighting a potential pathway for mammalian adaptation. These findings underscore the need for continued surveillance of evolving H5Nx viruses to mitigate emerging zoonotic threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the increasing zoonotic risk posed by emerging haemagglutinin (HA) variants of clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5Nx) high pathogenicity avian influenza viruses, which have shown cross-species adaptation, including outbreaks in dairy cattle.
Design and population
The study analyzed HA evolution in clade 2.3.4.4b H5Nx viruses from 2020 to 2025, focusing on key residues within the receptor-binding domain, in samples from various hosts including cattle and ferrets.
Core methods
The researchers used sequence analysis to identify immune escape mutations, particularly the HA E185K mutation, and conducted ferret studies to assess pathogenicity and transmission potential.
Main results
The study identified the HA E185K mutation as a novel immune escape mutation that enhances binding to avian- and human-type sialic acid receptors. However, ferret studies showed that this mutation did not confer transmission to direct or indirect contact ferrets.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting a potential pathway for mammalian adaptation, linking immune escape with expanded receptor specificity. This mutation is increasingly prevalent in cattle-derived H5N1 viruses and enhances binding to human-like receptors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for continued surveillance of evolving H5Nx viruses to mitigate emerging zoonotic threats, particularly in light of the potential for immune escape and receptor specificity expansion.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of ferret models, which may not fully represent human transmission dynamics. The evidence strength is moderate due to the use of animal models and the need for further human studies.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

高致病性禽流感 H5N1—迁徙和常驻 野生鸟类 中的物种特异性组织病理学和病毒抗原分布,德国南威斯特法伦州 2025

Highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1—species-specific histopathology and viral antigen distribution in migratory and resident wild birds, South Westphalia, Germany 2025
作者： Georg Beythien, Hannah Gerhards, Martin C. Peters, Jochen Kilwinski, Pauline Pöpperl, Inga Nägler, Ann Kathrin Ahrens, Anne Pohlmann, Martin Beer, Wolfgang Baumgärtner
摘要中文翻译2025年底,德国面临高致病性流感病毒亚型H5N1进化枝2.3.4.4b的主要暴发,导致迁徙和留鸟野生鸟类和家禽中的死亡率较高。我们检查了 16 只死去的鸟类,包括 2025 年 10 月至 12 月在德国西部发现的欧亚鹤、鹅、水鸭和猛禽。在这里,我们使用组织学和免疫组织化学测定的病毒抗原分布以及潜在的病毒 系统发育 来对光学显微镜结果进行比较分析。病变和病毒抗原分布因物种而异,鹤显示出广泛的器官感染,包括中枢神经系统(CNS)、胰腺、脾脏、肝脏和肾脏,而其他物种则表现出更有针对性的器官受累。系统发育分析确定了 EA-2024-DI.2.1 亚系,其中鹤形成一个单系簇,这与鹤的共享栖息/迁徙行为一致,尽管流行病学联系尚未得到正式证明。总之,我们的研究结果表明物种特异性病变模式和病毒组织向性,这可能会影响 传播 动力学和环境脱落。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查 2025 年德国 暴发 期间迁徙和留鸟 野生鸟类 中 高致病性禽流感 H5N1 的物种特异性组织病理学和病毒抗原分布,强调需要了解不同鸟类物种如何促进病毒的传播和影响。
设计与对象
该研究对 2025 年 10 月至 12 月期间从德国西部收集的 16 只死亡鸟类进行了检查,包括欧亚鹤、鹅、水鸭和猛禽。这些鸟类属于高致病性流感病毒 H5N1 亚型 2.3.4.4b 亚型 暴发 的一部分。
核心方法
研究人员使用光学显微镜和免疫组织化学进行了比较分析,以评估组织病理学和病毒抗原分布,同时进行系统发育分析以确定潜在的病毒谱系。
主要结果
病变和病毒抗原分布因物种而异,鹤显示出广泛的器官感染,包括中枢神经系统(CNS)、胰腺、脾脏、肝脏和肾脏,而其他物种则表现出更有针对性的器官受累。系统发育分析确定了 EA-2024-DI.2.1 亚系,与鹤形成单系簇。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,物种特异性病变模式和病毒组织趋向性可能会影响 传播 动态和环境脱落,为不同鸟类如何与病毒相互作用提供新见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以通过强调不同器官受累和 传播 模式的潜力,为 禽流感 暴发 的策略和风险评估提供信息,特别是在候鸟种群中。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其关注单个 暴发 和相对较小的样本量而受到限制,但由于进行了详细的组织病理学和系统发育分析,证据是强有力的。

Highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1—species-specific histopathology and viral antigen distribution in migratory and resident wild birds, South Westphalia, Germany 2025

Authors: Georg Beythien, Hannah Gerhards, Martin C. Peters, Jochen Kilwinski, Pauline Pöpperl, Inga Nägler, Ann Kathrin Ahrens, Anne Pohlmann, Martin Beer, Wolfgang Baumgärtner
Original abstract (English)
In late 2025, Germany faced a major outbreak of highly pathogenic influenza virus subtype H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b, causing high mortality in migratory and resident wild birds and poultry. We examined 16 deceased birds, including Eurasian cranes, geese, a teal, and raptors found in western Germany between October and December 2025. Here, we provide a comparative analysis of light microscopic findings using histology and viral antigen distribution determined by immunohistochemistry, alongside the underlying viral phylogeny. Lesions and viral antigen distribution varied by species, with cranes showing infection in a broad organ spectrum, including the central nervous system (CNS), pancreas, spleen, liver, and kidneys, whereas other species exhibited a more targeted organ involvement. Phylogenetic analysis identified the EA-2024-DI.2.1 sublineage, with cranes forming a monophyletic cluster, which was consistent with the shared roosting/migratory behavior of cranes, although epidemiologic links were not formally demonstrated. In summary, our findings indicate species-specific lesion patterns and viral tissue tropism, which may influence transmission dynamics and environmental shedding.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the species-specific histopathology and viral antigen distribution of highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 in migratory and resident wild birds during an outbreak in Germany in 2025, highlighting the need to understand how different bird species may contribute to the spread and impact of the virus.
Design and population
The study involved examining 16 deceased birds, including Eurasian cranes, geese, a teal, and raptors, collected from western Germany between October and December 2025. These birds were part of an outbreak of highly pathogenic influenza virus subtype H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b.
Core methods
The researchers conducted a comparative analysis using light microscopy and immunohistochemistry to assess histopathology and viral antigen distribution, alongside phylogenetic analysis to determine the underlying viral lineage.
Main results
Lesions and viral antigen distribution varied by species, with cranes showing infection in a broad organ spectrum, including the central nervous system (CNS), pancreas, spleen, liver, and kidneys, whereas other species exhibited a more targeted organ involvement. Phylogenetic analysis identified the EA-2024-DI.2.1 sublineage, with cranes forming a monophyletic cluster.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that species-specific lesion patterns and viral tissue tropism may influence transmission dynamics and environmental shedding, offering new insights into how different bird species interact with the virus.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform surveillance strategies and risk assessments for avian influenza outbreaks, particularly in migratory bird populations, by highlighting the potential for varied organ involvement and transmission patterns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single outbreak and a relatively small sample size, but the evidence is strong due to the detailed histopathological and phylogenetic analyses conducted.

中国广东 禽流感 H14 病毒的遗传特征:洲际 传播 和东亚-澳大利亚迁飞路线重配的证据。

Genetic Characterization of Avian Influenza H14 Viruses in Guangdong, China: Evidence of Intercontinental Transmission and Reassortment in the East Asian-Australasian Flyway.
作者： Jie Liu, Yuan-Guo Li, Ruo Huang, Qiu-Cheng Yao, Qiong Yang, Xue-Feng Shen, Ye Ge, Yu-Wei Gao
摘要中文翻译摘要 H14 禽流感 病毒(AIV)非常罕见,有关其在中国大陆传播的数据有限。本研究调查了中国南方 H14 病毒的生态起源和人畜共患潜力。活动《监测》在广东开展(2022-2025)。对分离株进行了全 基因组 测序和系统发育分析。使用固相测定评估禽类和人型受体的受体结合特异性。在禽类、哺乳动物和人类细胞系中评估了体外复制。在小鼠中评估了体内致病性。在这项研究中,分离出四种 H14 AIV(一种 H14N6、两种 H14N7 和一种 H14N8)。我们发现了一个新的 H14 传播 网络,涉及 Mareca penelope 和多种滨鸟物种(Tringa、Himantopus),这表明存在多宿主传播机制。从系统发育上看,广东分离株在欧亚谱系中形成了一个独特的分支,与俄罗斯和柬埔寨的菌株密切相关。重要的是,三个分离株(SZ3742/H14N6、ZJ3523/H14N7 和 SZ3536/H14N8)表现出双重受体结合特异性,可识别鸟类型(α-2,3)和人类型(α-2,6)唾液酸。人 A549 细胞中的复制具有菌株依赖性:SZ3742/H14N6 达到的峰值滴度与 DF-1 细胞中相当,但低于 MDCK 细胞中的峰值滴度,而 ZJ3523/H14N7 和 SZ3536/H14N8 达到的峰值滴度低于两个参考细胞系。尽管在人类 A549 细胞中的适应性降低,所有三种测试的分离株均在小鼠肺部以低滴度短暂复制,病毒致病性有限。分子表征揭示了关键的哺乳动物适应标记和典型的 HA 裂解位点基序 (PDKQTR↓GLF)。这项研究提供了H14 AIV在中国广东省流行的第一个证据。该病毒在候鸟中表现出广泛的 宿主 范围,并拥有与哺乳动物适应相关的遗传标记,突出表明东亚-澳大利亚迁徙路线需要增加 监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查 H14 禽流感 病毒在中国南方的生态起源和人畜共患潜力,因为这些病毒很罕见,并且有关其在中国大陆传播的数据有限。
设计与对象
该研究于2022年至2025年在中国广东省进行,其中进行了活性监测分离H14 禽流感病毒。在此期间分离出四种 H14 AIV(一种 H14N6、两种 H14N7 和一种 H14N8)。
核心方法
对分离株进行全 基因组 测序和系统发育分析。使用固相测定评估受体结合特异性,并评估禽类、哺乳动物和人类细胞系的体外复制。在小鼠中评估了体内致病性。
主要结果
该研究发现了一个涉及 Mareca penelope 和多种滨鸟(Tringa、Himantopus)的新型 H14 传播 网络,表明存在多宿主传播机制。三种分离株(SZ3742/H14N6、ZJ3523/H14N7 和 SZ3536/H14N8)表现出双重受体结合特异性,可识别鸟类型(α-2,3)和人类型(α-2,6)唾液酸。所有三种测试分离株均在小鼠肺部以低滴度短暂复制,病毒致病性有限。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了与哺乳动物适应相关的遗传标记和典型的 HA 裂解位点基序的存在,表明 传播 具有跨物种的潜力。这项研究提供了H14 AIV在中国广东省流行的第一个证据。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了东亚-澳大利亚迁飞路线中 监测 增加的重要性,因为该病毒在候鸟中表现出广泛的 宿主 范围,并拥有与哺乳动物适应相关的遗传标记。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对特定地理区域的关注以及体外和体内模型的使用而受到限制,这可能无法完全代表自然的 传播 动力学。证据是中等的,因为它基于分子表征和有限的 宿主 复制研究。

Genetic Characterization of Avian Influenza H14 Viruses in Guangdong, China: Evidence of Intercontinental Transmission and Reassortment in the East Asian-Australasian Flyway.

Authors: Jie Liu, Yuan-Guo Li, Ruo Huang, Qiu-Cheng Yao, Qiong Yang, Xue-Feng Shen, Ye Ge, Yu-Wei Gao
Original abstract (English)
Abstract H14 avian influenza viruses (AIVs) are rare, with limited data on their circulation in mainland China. This study investigated the ecological origins and zoonotic potential of H14 viruses in southern China. Active surveillance was conducted in Guangdong (2022-2025). The isolates underwent full-genome sequencing and phylogenetic analysis. Receptor binding specificity for avian- and human-type receptors was evaluated using a solid-phase assay. In vitro replication was assessed in avian, mammalian, and human cell lines. In vivo pathogenicity was evaluated in mice. In this study, four H14 AIVs (one H14N6, two H14N7, and one H14N8) were isolated. We identified a novel H14 transmission network involving Mareca penelope and multiple shorebird species ( Tringa , Himantopus ), suggesting a multihost dispersal mechanism. Phylogenetically, the Guangdong isolates formed a distinct subclade within the Eurasian lineage, closely related to strains from Russia and Cambodia. Importantly, the three isolates (SZ3742/H14N6, ZJ3523/H14N7, and SZ3536/H14N8) exhibited dual receptor-binding specificity, recognising both avian-type (α-2,3) and human-type (α-2,6) sialic acids. Replication in human A549 cells was strain-dependent: SZ3742/H14N6 reached a peak titre comparable to that in DF-1 cells but lower than that in MDCK cells, whereas ZJ3523/H14N7 and SZ3536/H14N8 reached lower peak titres than in both reference cell lines. All three tested isolates replicated transiently at low titers in mouse lungs with limited viral pathogenicity, despite reduced fitness in human A549 cells. Molecular characterization revealed key mammalian adaptation markers and a typical HA cleavage site motif (PDKQTR↓GLF). This study provides the first evidence of H14 AIV circulation in Guangdong Province, China. The virus demonstrates a broad host range in migratory birds and possesses genetic markers associated with mammalian adaptation, highlighting the need for increased surveillance in the East Asian‒Australasian Flyway.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the ecological origins and zoonotic potential of H14 avian influenza viruses in southern China, as these viruses are rare and have limited data on their circulation in mainland China.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Guangdong, China, from 2022 to 2025, where active surveillance was carried out to isolate H14 avian influenza viruses. Four H14 AIVs (one H14N6, two H14N7, and one H14N8) were isolated during this period.
Core methods
The isolates were subjected to full-genome sequencing and phylogenetic analysis. Receptor binding specificity was evaluated using a solid-phase assay, and in vitro replication was assessed in avian, mammalian, and human cell lines. In vivo pathogenicity was evaluated in mice.
Main results
The study identified a novel H14 transmission network involving Mareca penelope and multiple shorebird species (Tringa, Himantopus), suggesting a multihost dispersal mechanism. Three isolates (SZ3742/H14N6, ZJ3523/H14N7, and SZ3536/H14N8) exhibited dual receptor-binding specificity, recognising both avian-type (α-2,3) and human-type (α-2,6) sialic acids. All three tested isolates replicated transiently at low titers in mouse lungs with limited viral pathogenicity.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the presence of genetic markers associated with mammalian adaptation and a typical HA cleavage site motif, indicating potential for cross-species transmission. This study provides the first evidence of H14 AIV circulation in Guangdong Province, China.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the importance of increased surveillance in the East Asian‒Australasian Flyway, as the virus demonstrates a broad host range in migratory birds and possesses genetic markers linked to mammalian adaptation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a specific geographic region and the use of in vitro and in vivo models, which may not fully represent natural transmission dynamics. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on molecular characterization and limited host replication studies.

泛藏高原地区高致病性禽流感风险时空变化及影响因素.

Spatiotemporal variations and influencing factors of highly pathogenic avian influenza risk in the Pan-Tibetan Plateau region.
作者： Hong-Yan Ren, Yao Wang, Run-He Shi
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 高致病性禽流感(HPAI)是一种严重威胁养禽业和公众健康的重大传染病。然而,青藏高原及其周边地区是候鸟的重要生态走廊,时空模式和驱动力仍然难以捉摸。本研究旨在确定驱动这些变化的因素,并评估该地区 野生鸟类 和家禽的 HPAI 风险。方法基于联合国粮食及农业组织和官方兽医公报的高致病性禽流感记录以及泛藏高原(PTP)(包括西藏自治区(XZ)、青海省(QH)、甘肃省(GS)、四川省(SC)、云南省(YN)和新疆维吾尔自治区(XJ)的34个潜在驱动因素,本研究利用MaxEnt模型模拟高致病性禽流感风险的空间格局野生鸟类、家禽以及两者的整合,并确定关键风险因素。结果 野生鸟类 模型和家禽模型的性能都很稳健(测试 AUC 值均 > 0.9)。尽管混合模型总体表现尚可,但它显着低估了 野生鸟类 的感染风险。野生鸟类 HPAI高风险区集中在XZ、QH、GS和XJ(>93%),与坡度、距水源地距离等自然因素有密切关系。相反,家禽主要分布在延宁、南卡罗来纳、佐治亚(约占70%)和省会城市周边,以国内生产总值和道路网络等社会经济变量为主。此外,在 GS、XJ、XZ 和 QH 地区发现了重叠的高致病性禽流感风险区域,这可能与家禽产量增加和湿地景观破碎有关。此外,建议按 宿主 类型对 HPAI 风险建模进行分层,特别是在 监测 数据稀疏的区域。结论 野生鸟类 和家禽 HPAI 风险分别受自然因素和社会经济条件影响,且高风险区域重叠,需重点关注。本研究有助于理解不同宿主中HPAI 传播机制,为PTP中差异化监测提供科学依据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定驱动泛藏高原地区 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 风险时空变化的因素,满足了解 野生鸟类 和家禽中 HPAI 传播 机制的需要。
设计与对象
该研究基于联合国粮食及农业组织和官方兽医的高致病性禽流感记录,利用 MaxEnt 模型模拟了 2010 年至 2020 年西藏自治区 (XZ)、青海省 (QH)、甘肃省 (GS)、四川省 (SC)、云南省 (YN) 和新疆维吾尔自治区 (XJ) 的 野生鸟类 和家禽的高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 风险。公告。
核心方法
MaxEnt 模型用于模拟 野生鸟类 和家禽 HPAI 风险的空间模式,纳入 34 个潜在驱动因素,并按 宿主 类型对模型进行分层。
主要结果
野生鸟类和家禽模型表现稳健(测试AUC值>0.9),野生鸟类的高风险区域集中在XZ、QH、GS和XJ,家禽主要分布在YN、SC、GS和省会城市周边。
解释与创新
该研究确定自然因素和社会经济条件分别是 野生鸟类 和家禽 HPAI 风险的主要驱动因素,并强调需要在重叠的高风险地区重点关注 野生鸟类。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果为泛青高原地区差异化监测提供了科学依据,有助于理解不同宿主中HPAI 传播机制。
局限与证据强度
该研究的混合模型显着低估了 野生鸟类 的感染风险,并且研究结果受到某些地区 野生鸟类 数据可用性的限制。

Spatiotemporal variations and influencing factors of highly pathogenic avian influenza risk in the Pan-Tibetan Plateau region.

Authors: Hong-Yan Ren, Yao Wang, Run-He Shi
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) is a major infectious disease threatening the poultry industry and public health severely. The spatiotemporal patterns and driving forces, however, remain elusive in the Tibetan Plateau and its vicinity, an ecologically critical corridor for migratory birds. This study aims to identify the factors driving these variations and to assess the HPAI risk in wild birds and domestic poultry across this region. Methods Based on HPAI records from the Food and Agriculture Organization of the United Nations and the Official Veterinary Bulletin and 34 potential drivers in the Pan-Tibetan Plateau (PTP), comprising Xizang Autonomous Region (XZ), Qinghai Province (QH), Gansu Province (GS), Sichuan Province (SC), Yunnan Province (YN) and Xinjiang Uygur Autonomous Region (XJ), this study utilized the MaxEnt models to simulate spatial patterns of HPAI risk of wild birds, domestic poultry, and the integration of both, and identify key risk factors. Results Performances of models for wild birds and domestic poultry models were robust (both Test AUC values > 0.9). Although the mixed model performed acceptably overall, it significantly underestimated the infection risk in wild birds. High-risk areas of wild birds HPAI were concentrated in XZ, QH, GS and XJ (> 93%), showing a close relationship with natural factors like slope and distance to water sources. Conversely, those for domestic poultry were mainly distributed in YN, SC, GS (about 70%) and the peripheries of provincial capitals, dominated by socioeconomic variables such as gross domestic product and road networks. Moreover, overlapping HPAI risk areas were found in GS, XJ, XZ, and QH, which may be associated with increasing poultry production and fragmented wetland landscape. Additionally, it’s recommended that HPAI risk modeling be stratified by host types, particularly in areas with sparse surveillance data. Conclusions HPAI risks in wild birds and domestic poultry are dominated by natural factors and socioeconomic conditions respectively, and overlapping high-risk areas require focused attention. This study contributes to the understanding of HPAI transmission mechanisms in different hosts, providing a scientific basis for differentiated surveillance in the PTP.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to identify factors driving the spatiotemporal variations of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) risk in the Pan-Tibetan Plateau region, addressing the need for understanding HPAI transmission mechanisms in wild birds and domestic poultry.
Design and population
The study utilized MaxEnt models to simulate HPAI risk in wild birds and domestic poultry across Xizang Autonomous Region (XZ), Qinghai Province (QH), Gansu Province (GS), Sichuan Province (SC), Yunnan Province (YN), and Xinjiang Uygur Autonomous Region (XJ) from 2010 to 2020, based on HPAI records from the Food and Agriculture Organization of the United Nations and the Official Veterinary Bulletin.
Core methods
MaxEnt models were used to simulate spatial patterns of HPAI risk for wild birds and domestic poultry, incorporating 34 potential drivers, and stratifying the models by host types.
Main results
The models for wild birds and domestic poultry showed robust performances (Test AUC values > 0.9), with high-risk areas for wild birds concentrated in XZ, QH, GS, and XJ, and for domestic poultry mainly distributed in YN, SC, GS, and the peripheries of provincial capitals.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified natural factors and socioeconomic conditions as the dominant drivers of HPAI risk in wild birds and domestic poultry, respectively, and highlighted the need for focused surveillance in overlapping high-risk areas.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a scientific basis for differentiated surveillance in the Pan-Tibetan Plateau region, contributing to the understanding of HPAI transmission mechanisms in different hosts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s mixed model significantly underestimated the infection risk in wild birds, and the findings are limited by the availability of surveillance data in some areas.

全球 禽流感 研究中普遍存在测序错误的证据。

Evidence of widespread sequencing errors in global avian influenza study.
作者： Michelle Wille, Ashley C. Banyard, Francesco Bonfante, Andrew C. Breed, Ian Hay Brown, Alice Fusaro, Erik A. Karlsson, Nicola S. Lewis, Martha I. Nelson, Leslie D. Sims
摘要中文翻译(2025) 1 提出了一项研究,评估来自不常采样国家的鸟粪样本是否存在 禽流感 病毒。样本采集自非洲(马达加斯加、莫桑比克、索马里)、中东(也门)、南亚(马尔代夫、斯里兰卡)、东南亚(印度尼西亚、缅甸、菲律宾)和大洋洲(巴布亚新几内亚)。该研究旨在解决全球 监测 的关键差距,并提供重要数据,以增强对区域病毒多样性和 进化 的了解,并为高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) 入侵风险评估提供信息。然而,这项研究提供的数据和得出的结论引起了全球流感界的高度关注。该研究所依据的病毒序列数据与长期以来对全球 禽流感 进化 的理解相矛盾,导致结论与全球证据基础不一致。在 GenBank 或 GISAID 等公共数据库中发布错误的病毒序列阻碍了全球背景下对数据的合理解释,导致误导性结论,影响疾病风险评估和政策决策。

局限与证据强度
然而,这项研究提供的数据和得出的结论引起了全球流感界的高度关注。

Evidence of widespread sequencing errors in global avian influenza study.

Authors: Michelle Wille, Ashley C. Banyard, Francesco Bonfante, Andrew C. Breed, Ian Hay Brown, Alice Fusaro, Erik A. Karlsson, Nicola S. Lewis, Martha I. Nelson, Leslie D. Sims
Original abstract (English)
(2025) 1 present a study whereby guano samples from infrequently sampled countries were assessed for the presence of avian influenza viruses. Samples were collected from Africa (Madagascar, Mozambique, Somalia), the Middle East (Yemen), South Asia (Maldives, Sri Lanka), South East Asia (Indonesia, Myanmar, the Philippines) and Oceania (Papua New Guinea). The study aims to address critical gaps in global surveillance and provide important data that could enhance understanding of regional viral diversity and evolution, as well as inform high pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) incursion risk assessments. However, the data presented and the conclusions drawn from this study are of high concern to the global influenza community. The viral sequence data on which the study is based contradict long-established understanding of global avian influenza evolution, resulting in conclusions that are incongruent with the global evidence base. The publication of erroneous viral sequences in public databases such as GenBank or GISAID hinders rational interpretation of data in a global context, resulting in misleading conclusions that affect disease risk assessments and policy decisions.

The study aims to address critical gaps in global surveillance and provide important data that could enhance understanding of regional viral diversity and evolution, as well as inform high pathogenicity avian influenza (HPAI) incursion risk assessments.
Design and population
(2025) 1 present a study whereby guano samples from infrequently sampled countries were assessed for the presence of avian influenza viruses.
Core methods
Samples were collected from Africa (Madagascar, Mozambique, Somalia), the Middle East (Yemen), South Asia (Maldives, Sri Lanka), South East Asia (Indonesia, Myanmar, the Philippines) and Oceania (Papua New Guinea).
Main results
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a directly observed main result.
Interpretation and novelty
The viral sequence data on which the study is based contradict long-established understanding of global avian influenza evolution, resulting in conclusions that are incongruent with the global evidence base.
Scientific and public-health significance
The publication of erroneous viral sequences in public databases such as GenBank or GISAID hinders rational interpretation of data in a global context, resulting in misleading conclusions that affect disease risk assessments and policy decisions.
Limitations and evidence strength
However, the data presented and the conclusions drawn from this study are of high concern to the global influenza community.

基于无人机的快速检测在H5N1禽流感暴发期间对白鹤(Grus grus)热点区域的识别中使用深度学习

Rapid UAV-based detection of crane (Grus grus) mortality hotspots during an H5N1 avian influenza outbreak using deep learning
作者： Yotam Nagar, Assaf Chen
摘要中文翻译在传染病暴发期间对野生动物热点区域进行快速检测对于生态风险缓解、人兽共患病风险管理和及时干预至关重要,但实地调查往往耗时、劳动密集且空间受限。本研究评估了结合深度学习的无人机(UAV)图像作为一种操作性工具,用于在以色列胡拉谷地H5N1禽流感暴发期间绘制存活和死亡的普通白鹤(Grus grus)的分布情况。高分辨率RGB图像在水生栖息地和陆地觅食栖息地采集,并进行标注以区分存活和死亡个体。基于YOLOv8的检测框架通过特定栖息地训练、多栖息地联合训练和从大型鸟类数据集进行的迁移学习进行了测试,同时结合选择性数据增强、滑动窗口推理和加权框融合技术。存活的白鹤在不同栖息地中被可靠检测,其在拼接觅食区图像中的AP@50达到0.89,在原始觅食区图像中的AP@50达到0.98。死亡的白鹤在水体中被准确检测(AP@50≈0.90),但在陆地觅食区的检测仍较为有限(AP@50≈0.64–0.70),这是基于空中标注而没有现场确认的结果。过采样、欠采样和焦点损失提供了有限的改进,表明仅类别不平衡并不能完全解释陆地检测性能较低的原因,而尸体稀缺、视觉多样性有限以及与异质地面背景的模糊性也可能有所贡献。多栖息地联合训练和迁移学习提高了对陆地罕见死亡白鹤类别的泛化能力。这些发现展示了基于无人机的深度学习在野生动物疾病暴发期间进行快速热点识别和评估的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在传染病暴发期间对野生动物热点区域进行快速检测对于生态风险缓解、人兽共患病风险管理和及时干预至关重要,但实地调查往往耗时、劳动密集且空间受限。
设计与对象
本研究评估了结合深度学习的无人机(UAV)图像作为一种操作性工具,用于在以色列胡拉谷地H5N1禽流感暴发期间绘制存活和死亡的普通白鹤(Grus grus)的分布情况。
核心方法
高分辨率RGB图像在水生栖息地和陆地觅食栖息地采集,并进行标注以区分存活和死亡个体。基于YOLOv8的检测框架通过特定栖息地训练、多栖息地联合训练和从大型鸟类数据集进行的迁移学习进行了测试,同时结合选择性数据增强、滑动窗口推理和加权框融合技术。
主要结果
存活的白鹤在不同栖息地中被可靠检测,其在拼接觅食区图像中的AP@50达到0.89,在原始觅食区图像中的AP@50达到0.98。死亡的白鹤在水体中被准确检测(AP@50≈0.90),但在陆地觅食区的检测仍较为有限(AP@50≈0.64–0.70),这是基于空中标注而没有现场确认的结果。
解释与创新
这些发现展示了基于无人机的深度学习在野生动物疾病暴发期间进行快速热点识别和评估的价值。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为禽流感 暴发期间野生动物死亡率快速死亡率工具的开发提供信息,有助于缓解生态风险和人畜共患病风险管理。
局限与证据强度
过采样、欠采样和焦点损失提供了有限的改进,表明仅类别不平衡并不能完全解释陆地检测性能较低的原因,而尸体稀缺、视觉多样性有限以及与异质地面背景的模糊性也可能有所贡献。

Rapid UAV-based detection of crane (Grus grus) mortality hotspots during an H5N1 avian influenza outbreak using deep learning

Authors: Yotam Nagar, Assaf Chen
Original abstract (English)
Rapid detection of wildlife mortality during infectious disease outbreaks is essential for ecological risk mitigation, zoonotic-risk management, and timely intervention, yet field surveys are often slow, labor-intensive, and spatially limited. This study evaluates unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) imagery combined with deep learning as an operational surveillance tool for mapping live and deceased Common Cranes ( Grus grus ) during a highly pathogenic avian influenza (H5N1) outbreak in the Hula Valley, Israel. High-resolution RGB imagery was collected over aquatic roosting and terrestrial feeding habitats and annotated to distinguish living and dead individuals. A YOLOv8-based detection framework was tested using habitat-specific training, combined multi-habitat training, and transfer learning from a large avian dataset, together with selective data augmentation, sliding-window inference, and Weighted Boxes Fusion. Living cranes were detected reliably across habitats, with AP@50 reaching 0.89 in mosaicked feeding-area imagery and 0.98 in raw feeding-area imagery. Dead cranes were detected accurately in water bodies (AP@50 ≈ 0.90), but detection in terrestrial feeding areas remained more limited (AP@50 ≈ 0.64–0.70), based on aerial annotations without in situ confirmation. Oversampling, undersampling, and focal loss provided limited improvement, suggesting that class imbalance alone does not fully explain the lower terrestrial detection performance, and that carcass scarcity, limited visual diversity, and ambiguity against heterogeneous ground backgrounds may also contribute. Joint multi-habitat training and transfer learning improved generalization for the rare dead-crane class on land. These findings demonstrate the value of UAV-based deep learning for rapid mortality assessment and hotspot identification during wildlife disease outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rapid detection of wildlife mortality during infectious disease outbreaks is essential for ecological risk mitigation, zoonotic-risk management, and timely intervention, yet field surveys are often slow, labor-intensive, and spatially limited.
Design and population

A YOLOv8-based detection framework was tested using habitat-specific training, combined multi-habitat training, and transfer learning from a large avian dataset, together with selective data augmentation, sliding-window inference, and Weighted Boxes Fusion.
Main results
Living cranes were detected reliably across habitats, with AP@50 reaching 0.89 in mosaicked feeding-area imagery and 0.98 in raw feeding-area imagery. Dead cranes were detected accurately in water bodies (AP@50 ≈ 0.90), but detection in terrestrial feeding areas remained more limited (AP@50 ≈ 0.64–0.70), based on aerial annotations without in situ confirmation.
Interpretation and novelty

The findings can inform the development of rapid surveillance tools for wildlife mortality during avian influenza outbreaks, aiding in ecological risk mitigation and zoonotic-risk management.
Limitations and evidence strength
Oversampling, undersampling, and focal loss provided limited improvement, suggesting that class imbalance alone does not fully explain the lower terrestrial detection performance, and that carcass scarcity, limited visual diversity, and ambiguity against heterogeneous ground backgrounds may also contribute.

H9N2 血凝素中的单个残基缺失将病毒负荷与疾病严重程度分开

A single-residue deletion in H9N2 hemagglutinin uncouples viral burden from disease severity
作者： Yixue Sun, Yanting Zhu, Pengju Zhang, Shouzhi Sheng, Lili Gai, Siqi Li, Fangyu Zhang, Peige Cao, Ao He, Weixia Wang
摘要中文翻译H9N2 禽流感 病毒具有显着的遗传多样性。血凝素 (HA) 受体结合域内的缺失代表了一个值得注意的、尽管罕见的进化轨迹。在这里,我们使用同基因反向遗传学 H9N2 病毒系统地表征了 HA 残基 227 处的单氨基酸缺失(指定为 HA Δ227 )的多方面影响,HA 是 220 环内自然发生的缺失,与关键抗原表位重叠。矛盾的是,HA Δ227 会带来适应性成本,并与增强的 HA 受体结合亲合力、降低明显的病毒体相关 NA 活性、改变 HA/NA 功能平衡、减弱体外病毒复制以及减少体内呼吸道脱落有关。值得注意的是,尽管复制适应性受损,但突变病毒的感染与鸡中 宿主 免疫应答 的改变有关,其特征是干扰素 γ (IFN-γ)、白细胞介素 10 (IL-10)、白细胞介素 1β (IL-1β) 和单核细胞趋化蛋白 1 (MCP-1) 水平升高。这些改变的 免疫应答 与更严重的肺部病变相关,表明疾病严重程度和病毒 RNA 载量之间存在关联。此外,缺失增加了HA介导的膜融合的pH阈值,并显着改变了抗原关系。总的来说,我们的研究结果将HA残基227确定为一个功能上重要的位点,其中单个残基缺失与复制能力和疾病严重程度之间的表型权衡相关:复制适应性降低,伴随着炎症反应改变和更大的组织损伤。这项研究强调了流感病毒 HA 中罕见缺失的潜在表型意义,并表明仅病毒负荷可能不足以预测疾病的严重程度。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 H9N2 禽流感 病毒中血凝素 (HA) 受体结合域残基 227 处的单个氨基酸缺失的影响,以解决有关此类缺失的表型意义的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究使用在残基 227 (HA Δ227) 处有单氨基酸缺失的同基因反向遗传学 H9N2 病毒,系统地表征了其对鸡的多方面影响。
核心方法
研究人员利用同基因反向遗传学产生了 HA Δ227 突变病毒,并表征了其对病毒复制、受体结合亲和力、NA 活性、免疫应答 和鸡疾病严重程度的影响。
主要结果
HA Δ227 缺失造成了健康成本,增强了受体结合亲和力,改变了 HA/NA 功能平衡,并减少了体外病毒复制和体内呼吸道脱落。尽管存在这些适应性成本,突变病毒仍与 宿主 免疫应答 的改变和更严重的肺部病变有关。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,HA 中的单个残基缺失可以将病毒负荷与疾病严重程度脱钩,强调了此类缺失的潜在表型意义以及使用病毒 RNA 负荷预测疾病严重程度的局限性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究深入了解了病毒适应度与疾病严重程度之间的复杂关系,可以为 监测、诊断 和 治疗 的 禽流感 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到使用单一病毒株和缺乏直接人类感染数据的限制,但研究结果是新颖的,并提供了对流感病毒 进化 表型权衡的更深入的了解。

A single-residue deletion in H9N2 hemagglutinin uncouples viral burden from disease severity

Authors: Yixue Sun, Yanting Zhu, Pengju Zhang, Shouzhi Sheng, Lili Gai, Siqi Li, Fangyu Zhang, Peige Cao, Ao He, Weixia Wang
Original abstract (English)
H9N2 avian influenza viruses are characterized by significant genetic diversity. Deletions within the hemagglutinin (HA) receptor-binding domain represent a notable, albeit rare, evolutionary trajectory. Here, we systematically characterized the multifaceted impact of a single-amino-acid deletion at residue 227 of HA (designated HA Δ227 ), a naturally occurring deletion within the 220-loop that overlaps a key antigenic epitope, using isogenic reverse genetics H9N2 viruses. Paradoxically, the HA Δ227 imposed a fitness cost and was associated with enhanced HA receptor-binding avidity, reduced apparent virion-associated NA activity, altered HA/NA functional balance, attenuated viral replication in vitro , and reduced respiratory shedding in vivo . Notably, despite this compromised replicative fitness, infection with the mutant virus was associated with an altered host immune response in chickens, characterized by elevated levels of interferon-γ (IFN-γ), interleukin-10 (IL-10), interleukin-1β (IL-1β), and monocyte chemoattractant protein-1 (MCP-1). These altered immune responses were associated with more severe pulmonary lesions, indicating a dissociation between disease severity and viral RNA load. Furthermore, the deletion increased the pH threshold for HA-mediated membrane fusion and substantially altered antigenic relationships. Collectively, our findings identify HA residue 227 as a functionally important site at which a single-residue deletion is associated with a phenotypic trade-off between replicative capacity and disease severity: reduced replicative fitness accompanied by altered inflammatory responses and greater tissue injury. This study highlights the potential phenotypic significance of a rare deletion in influenza virus HA and demonstrates that viral burden alone may not adequately predict disease severity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the impact of a single-amino-acid deletion at residue 227 of the hemagglutinin (HA) receptor-binding domain in H9N2 avian influenza viruses, addressing the knowledge gap regarding the phenotypic significance of such deletions.
Design and population
The study used isogenic reverse genetics H9N2 viruses with a single-amino-acid deletion at residue 227 (HA Δ227) to systematically characterize its multifaceted impact in chickens.
Core methods
The researchers utilized isogenic reverse genetics to generate the HA Δ227 mutant virus and characterized its impact on viral replication, receptor-binding avidity, NA activity, immune response, and disease severity in chickens.
Main results
The HA Δ227 deletion imposed a fitness cost, enhanced receptor-binding avidity, altered HA/NA functional balance, and reduced viral replication in vitro and respiratory shedding in vivo. Despite these fitness costs, the mutant virus was associated with an altered host immune response and more severe pulmonary lesions.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that a single-residue deletion in HA can uncouple viral burden from disease severity, highlighting the potential phenotypic significance of such deletions and the limitations of using viral RNA load to predict disease severity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides insights into the complex relationship between viral fitness and disease severity, which can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment strategies for avian influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of a single viral strain and the absence of direct human infection data, but the findings are novel and provide a deeper understanding of the phenotypic trade-offs in influenza virus evolution.

与减少家禽环境中 禽流感 感染相关的生物安全措施:对 One Health 影响的系统评价和荟萃分析

Biosecurity measures associated with reduced avian influenza infection in poultry settings: a systematic review and meta-analysis with One Health implications
作者： Dimitra V. Pouliopoulou, Victoria D’Alessandro, Rachel Watson, Marjan Saeedi, Nicole Billias, Joy C. MacDermid, Erin Miller, Heather McClinchey, Kieran L. Quinn, Saverio Stranges
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高致病性 禽流感 继续在家禽和其他动物群体中引起大规模流行病,人们持续关注人畜共患 跨物种溢出。
设计与对象
我们的目的是评估商业家禽养殖场和后院家禽环境中特定卫生、个人防护设备和生物安全措施与农场 禽流感 感染之间的关联。
核心方法
我们进行了系统回顾和荟萃分析。MEDLINE、Embase、Scopus、CINAHL、CENTRAL、CABI、Web of Science 和 ClinicalTrials.gov 的检索时间自创建之日起至 2025 年 12 月 11 日。
主要结果
符合条件的研究是实证研究,比较卫生、个人防护设备或生物安全实践的使用与不使用或不同遵守水平,并报告农场水平的 禽流感 感染状况。
解释与创新
实用的、以接触为中心的生物安全实践与较低的农场 禽流感 感染几率最一致。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持优先考虑中断直接 传播 路径的可行措施,同时考虑实施质量、当地背景和行为可行性。
局限与证据强度
研究间异质性因分析而异。证据的质量从低到中等。

Biosecurity measures associated with reduced avian influenza infection in poultry settings: a systematic review and meta-analysis with One Health implications

Authors: Dimitra V. Pouliopoulou, Victoria D’Alessandro, Rachel Watson, Marjan Saeedi, Nicole Billias, Joy C. MacDermid, Erin Miller, Heather McClinchey, Kieran L. Quinn, Saverio Stranges
Original abstract (English)
Background High pathogenicity avian influenza continues to cause large epizootics in poultry and other animal populations, with ongoing concern about zoonotic spillover. We aimed to estimate the association between specific hygiene, personal protective equipment, and biosecurity practices and farm-level avian influenza infection in commercial poultry farms and backyard poultry settings. Methods We conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis. MEDLINE, Embase, Scopus, CINAHL, CENTRAL, CABI, Web of Science, and ClinicalTrials.gov were searched from inception to Dec 11, 2025. Eligible studies were empirical studies that compared use versus non-use, or different adherence levels, of hygiene, personal protective equipment, or biosecurity practices and reported farm-level avian influenza infection status. Screening, data extraction and risk of bias assessment was completed by 2 independent reviewers. The main outcome was farm-level avian influenza infection. Odds ratios were pooled using random-effects models. The protocol was registered with PROSPERO, CRD420251174630. Findings The search identified 39 observational studies, including 28 case-control, nine cross-sectional, and two prospective cohort studies. Reduced odds of infection were observed for disinfection of equipment and premises overall (OR 0.37, 95% CI 0.14–0.99), human entry/exit decontamination stations (0.61, 0.40–0.94), personal hygiene practices (0.52, 0.35–0.76), contact-pathway PPE (0.67, 0.51–0.87), quarantine/cross-farm contamination control (0.49, 0.34–0.71), and biosecurity information resources (0.26, 0.07–0.91). Overall risk of bias was high in eight studies, some concerns in 24, and low in seven. Between-study heterogeneity varied across analyses. Certainty of evidence ranged from low to moderate. Interpretation Practical, contact-focused biosecurity practices were most consistently associated with lower farm-level avian influenza infection odds. Although the observational evidence does not establish causal effectiveness and may be affected by residual confounding, the findings support prioritising feasible measures that interrupt direct transmission pathways while accounting for implementation quality, local context, and behavioural feasibility.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
High pathogenicity avian influenza continues to cause large epizootics in poultry and other animal populations, with ongoing concern about zoonotic spillover.
Design and population

鸡鸭细胞中多种 高致病性禽流感 进化枝 2.3.4.4 病毒体外复制动力学的数学模型

Mathematical modeling of in vitro replication dynamics for multiple highly pathogenic avian influenza clade 2.3.4.4 viruses in chicken and duck cells
作者： Luca Bordes, Peter Hobbelen, Elena Blokker, Wim H. M. van der Poel, Catherine A. A. Beauchemin, Nancy Beerens, José L. Gonzales
摘要中文翻译摘要 家禽中 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 的引入、传播和后续检测受到病毒复制适应性、传播 适应性和毒力的影响。量化这些健康参数对于实施 监测 和家禽控制措施非常重要。本研究采用禽类体外模型,使用原代鸡胚成纤维细胞和鸭胚成纤维细胞来鉴定 2014 年至 2024 年间分离的 8 个主要 高致病性禽流感 分支 2.3.4.4 病毒基因型(H5N1、H5N8 和 H5N6)参考组的细胞适应性相关性。由于 HPAIV 在家禽中具有泛嗜性,因此使用原代成纤维细胞可以提供强大的适应性。评估不同家禽物种病毒适应性的系统模型。通过将纵向感染性病毒滴度和细胞致病性数据与数学模型相结合,将细胞群划分为易感区、潜伏区、感染区和死区,我们将“传统”病毒学参数(例如峰值病毒滴度和细胞死亡 50% 的时间)与机械参数(包括感染时间 ( t in f )、世代时间 ( t gen ) 和基本繁殖数 ( R 0 ))分开。我们的结果证实了 宿主 特定的健身特征与体内观察结果一致。这项研究强调了将禽类体外模型与数学模型相结合的潜力,以提供遗传相似病毒的高分辨率特征,从而有助于对新型 HPAIV 进行快速风险评估。重要性 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 家禽中的 高致病性禽流感 导致过去十年中数百万只动物被扑杀。高致病性禽流感在家禽中的引入和传播是由病毒的复制、传播和引起疾病的能力驱动的,而不同的家禽种类之间的病毒复制、传播和致病的能力不同。这些病毒适应性特征可以通过 突变 或重配快速改变,从而产生具有改变的引入风险和 传播 的新变体。在这项研究中,我们整合了体外和数学模型来评估遗传相似病毒的病毒适应性及其对两种重要家禽物种的风险。这种方法为传统动物模型提供了一种快速且可扩展的替代方案,符合 3R 原则,并支持对家禽新兴病毒变种的病毒适应性进行快速评估。这种方法可以很容易地扩展到包括人类在内的其他物种,并且适合评估所有病毒病原体的病毒适应性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解 高致病性禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 的复制适应性、传播 适应性和毒力如何影响其在家禽中的引入、传播和检测,因为这些性状因物种而异,并且可以通过 突变 或重配而迅速变化。
设计与对象
该研究使用原代鸡胚成纤维细胞和鸭胚成纤维细胞的体外模型来评估 2014 年至 2024 年间分离的八种主要 高致病性禽流感 进化枝 2.3.4.4 病毒基因型(H5N1、H5N8 和 H5N6)的适应性。
核心方法
研究人员将纵向感染性病毒滴度和细胞致病性数据与数学模型相结合,将细胞群分为易感区、潜伏区、感染区和死区,以区分传统病毒学参数和机械参数,例如感染时间、世代时间和基本繁殖数。
主要结果
结果证实了病毒的 宿主 特异性适应性特征与体内观察结果一致,突出了鸡和鸭细胞之间复制动态的差异。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证明了将禽类体外模型与数学模型相结合的潜力,可以为遗传相似病毒的适应性提供高分辨率的见解,从而支持对新出现的变种进行快速风险评估。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了一种可扩展且快速的方法来评估 HPAIV 在家禽中的适应性,这可以为 监测 和控制策略提供信息,以减轻 暴发 和扑杀的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外模型,并未考虑体内的复杂性,但数学模型与实验数据的整合为表征病毒适应性特征提供了中等程度的信心。

Mathematical modeling of in vitro replication dynamics for multiple highly pathogenic avian influenza clade 2.3.4.4 viruses in chicken and duck cells

Authors: Luca Bordes, Peter Hobbelen, Elena Blokker, Wim H. M. van der Poel, Catherine A. A. Beauchemin, Nancy Beerens, José L. Gonzales
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The introduction, spread, and subsequent detection of highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) in poultry are influenced by the virus replication fitness, transmission fitness, and virulence. Quantifying these fitness parameters is important for implementing surveillance and control measures for poultry. This study employs an avian in vitro model using primary chicken embryo fibroblast and duck embryo fibroblast to identify cellular correlates of fitness for a reference panel of eight dominant, highly pathogenic avian influenza clade 2.3.4.4 virus genotypes (H5N1, H5N8, and H5N6) isolated between 2014 and 2024. Because HPAIVs are pantropic in poultry, the use of primary fibroblasts could provide a robust systemic model to assess virus fitness in different poultry species. By integrating longitudinal infectious virus titers and cytopathogenicity data with a mathematical model, which delineates cell populations into susceptible, latent, infectious, and dead compartments, we disentangled “traditional” virological parameters such as peak virus titer and time to 50% cell death from mechanistic parameters, including the infecting time ( t i n f ), generation time ( t g e n ), and basic reproduction number ( R 0 ). Our results confirm host-specific fitness characteristics consistent with in vivo observations. This study underscores the potential of integrating avian in vitro models with mathematical modeling to provide high-resolution characterization of genetically similar viruses, building toward rapid risk assessments of novel HPAIVs. IMPORTANCE Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) outbreaks in poultry have resulted in the culling of millions of animals over the past decade. The introduction and spread of HPAI in poultry are driven by the ability of the virus to replicate, transmit, and cause disease, which varies among different poultry species. These viral fitness traits can change quickly through mutations or reassortment, leading to new variants with an altered risk of introduction and transmission. In this study, we integrated an in vitro and mathematical model to assess the viral fitness of genetically similar viruses and the risk they represent for two important poultry species. This approach offers a rapid and scalable alternative to traditional animal models, aligning with the 3R’s principle and supporting rapid assessment of the viral fitness of emerging virus variants for poultry. This approach can be easily expanded to other species, including humans, and is suitable to assess the viral fitness of all viral pathogens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand how the replication fitness, transmission fitness, and virulence of highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses (HPAIVs) influence their introduction, spread, and detection in poultry, as these traits vary among species and can change rapidly through mutations or reassortment.
Design and population
The study used an in vitro model with primary chicken embryo fibroblast and duck embryo fibroblast cells to assess the fitness of eight dominant, highly pathogenic avian influenza clade 2.3.4.4 virus genotypes (H5N1, H5N8, and H5N6) isolated between 2014 and 2024.
Core methods
The researchers integrated longitudinal infectious virus titers and cytopathogenicity data with a mathematical model that categorized cell populations into susceptible, latent, infectious, and dead compartments to differentiate traditional virological parameters from mechanistic ones such as infecting time, generation time, and basic reproduction number.
Main results
The results confirmed host-specific fitness characteristics of the viruses that align with in vivo observations, highlighting differences in replication dynamics between chicken and duck cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as demonstrating the potential of combining avian in vitro models with mathematical modeling to provide high-resolution insights into the fitness of genetically similar viruses, supporting rapid risk assessments for emerging variants.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a scalable and rapid method for assessing the fitness of HPAIVs in poultry, which can inform surveillance and control strategies to mitigate the risk of outbreaks and culling.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro models and does not account for in vivo complexities, but the integration of mathematical modeling with experimental data provides a moderate level of confidence in characterizing viral fitness traits.

人参皂苷 Rg1 作为佐剂增强 H9N2 禽流感 灭活 疫苗 免疫鸡的免疫力

Ginsenoside Rg1 as an adjuvant enhances immunity in chickens immunized with the H9N2 avian influenza inactivated vaccine
作者： Peng-Ju Zhang, Hong-Tao Wang, Meng-Man Li, Xuefeng Lyu, 高湛翱, Wei-Xia Wang, Xin-Tao Li, Li-Chun Zhang, Zhan-Ao Gao
摘要中文翻译为了研究人参皂苷 Rg1 作为 H9N2 禽流感 疫苗 佐剂的潜在用途,该研究检查了 Rg1 对灭活的 H9N2 疫苗 的免疫调节作用。总共 150 只一日龄未免疫 Arbor Acres (AA) 鸡被随机分配到三组(每组 n = 50):一组接受 H9N2 灭活 疫苗,另一组接受 Rg1 和 疫苗,对照组用 PBS 处理。每组均接受 3 次免疫接种,间隔 2 周。采集血清样本来测量 IgG 抗体水平和血凝抑制 (HI) 滴度。使用细胞计数试剂盒8和流式细胞术评估淋巴细胞增殖。通过 ELISA 定量血清细胞因子的水平,包括干扰素-γ (IFN-γ)、肿瘤坏死因子-α (TNF-α)、白细胞介素 (IL)-4 和 IL-6。最终免疫后十四天,用 H9N2 病毒株攻击鸡,并测量肺和肾中的病毒滴度。结果表明,接受 Rg1 和 疫苗 的组的特异性 IgG 和 HI 滴度显着增加。此外,该组显示出 T 细胞增殖增强和细胞因子产生增加,从而增强细胞免疫。重要的是,免疫保护结果表明,Rg1 和 疫苗 的组合显着提高了鸡对病毒株的保护,导致病毒脱落显着减少。这些发现表明,Rg1 可能增强 免疫应答 和 H9N2 灭活 疫苗 的保护功效,使其成为 H9N2 疫苗接种 的一种有前途的新佐剂。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨人参皂苷 Rg1 作为佐剂增强 免疫应答 对 H9N2 禽流感 灭活 疫苗 的潜力。
设计与对象
总共 150 只一日龄未免疫 Arbor Acres 鸡被随机分为三组:一组接受 H9N2 灭活 疫苗,另一组接受 Rg1 和 疫苗,对照组用 PBS 处理。
核心方法
该研究使用细胞计数试剂盒-8和流式细胞术来评估淋巴细胞增殖,并使用ELISA来量化血清细胞因子水平,包括IFN-γ、TNF-α、IL-4和IL-6。最终免疫后十四天,用 H9N2 病毒株攻击鸡,并测量肺和肾中的病毒滴度。
主要结果
接受 Rg1 和 疫苗 的组显示特异性 IgG 和 HI 滴度显着增加。该研究组还表现出 T 细胞增殖增强和细胞因子产生增加,表明细胞免疫得到改善。Rg1 和 疫苗 的组合显着改善了对病毒株的保护,从而显着减少了病毒脱落。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为 Rg1 可能增强 免疫应答 和 H9N2 灭活 疫苗 的保护功效的证据,表明它是 H9N2 疫苗接种 的一种有前途的新佐剂。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,Rg1 可能是提高 H9N2 禽流感 疫苗 有效性的有价值的佐剂,这可能有助于更好地控制家禽疾病,并有可能减少人畜共患 传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于动物模型,不包括人类数据,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据是温和的,因为它基于仅摘要的报告。

Ginsenoside Rg1 as an adjuvant enhances immunity in chickens immunized with the H9N2 avian influenza inactivated vaccine

Authors: Peng-Ju Zhang, Hong-Tao Wang, Meng-Man Li, Xuefeng Lyu, 高湛翱, Wei-Xia Wang, Xin-Tao Li, Li-Chun Zhang, Zhan-Ao Gao
Original abstract (English)
To investigate the potential use of Ginsenoside Rg1 as an adjuvant for the H9N2 avian influenza vaccine, the study examined Rg1’s immunomodulatory effects on an inactivated H9N2 vaccine. A total of 150 one-day-old non-immunized Arbor Acres (AA) chickens were randomly assigned to three groups ( n = 50 each): one receiving the H9N2 inactivated vaccine, another receiving Rg1 alongside the vaccine, and a control group treated with PBS. Each group underwent three immunization sessions spaced 2 weeks apart. Serum samples were taken to measure IgG antibody levels and hemagglutination inhibition (HI) titers. Lymphocyte proliferation was assessed using cell counting kits-8 and flow cytometry. The levels of serum cytokines, including Interferon-γ (IFN-γ), tumor necrosis factor-alpha (TNF-α), interleukin (IL)-4, and IL-6, were quantified via ELISA. Fourteen days post-final immunization, the chickens were challenged with the H9N2 strain, and viral titers in the lungs and kidneys were measured. Findings indicated that the group receiving Rg1 with the vaccine exhibited a notable increase in specific IgG and HI titers. Furthermore, this group showed enhanced T cell proliferation and elevated cytokine production, thereby bolstering cellular immunity. Importantly, the immune protection results revealed that the combination of Rg1 and the vaccine significantly improved the chickens’ protection against viral strains, leading to a marked reduction in viral shedding. These findings imply that Rg1 may enhance the immune response and protective efficacy of the H9N2 inactivated vaccine, positioning it as a promising new adjuvant for H9N2 vaccination.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the potential of Ginsenoside Rg1 as an adjuvant to enhance the immune response to the H9N2 avian influenza inactivated vaccine.
Design and population
A total of 150 one-day-old non-immunized Arbor Acres chickens were randomly assigned to three groups: one received the H9N2 inactivated vaccine, another received Rg1 with the vaccine, and a control group was treated with PBS.
Core methods
The study used cell counting kits-8 and flow cytometry to assess lymphocyte proliferation, and ELISA to quantify serum cytokine levels, including IFN-γ, TNF-α, IL-4, and IL-6. Fourteen days post-final immunization, chickens were challenged with the H9N2 strain, and viral titers in the lungs and kidneys were measured.
Main results
The group receiving Rg1 with the vaccine showed a notable increase in specific IgG and HI titers. This group also demonstrated enhanced T cell proliferation and elevated cytokine production, indicating improved cellular immunity. The combination of Rg1 and the vaccine significantly improved protection against viral strains, resulting in a marked reduction in viral shedding.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that Rg1 may enhance the immune response and protective efficacy of the H9N2 inactivated vaccine, suggesting it as a promising new adjuvant for H9N2 vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that Rg1 could be a valuable adjuvant for improving the effectiveness of H9N2 avian influenza vaccines, which may have implications for better disease control in poultry and potentially reduce zoonotic transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an animal model and does not include human data, which may affect the generalizability of findings. The evidence is moderate as it is based on an abstract-only report.

通过数字 PCR 区分废水中的禽流感病毒和季节性流感病毒。

Differentiating Avian and Seasonal Influenza Viruses in Wastewater by Digital PCR.
作者： Valentina Paracchini, Mauro Petrillo, Francisco Mayo Leon, Gerhard Buttinger, Sabrina Marciano, Marika Crimaudo, Calogero Terregino, Carolina Veneri, Giuseppina La Rosa, Elisabetta Suffredini

Differentiating Avian and Seasonal Influenza Viruses in Wastewater by Digital PCR.

Authors: Valentina Paracchini, Mauro Petrillo, Francisco Mayo Leon, Gerhard Buttinger, Sabrina Marciano, Marika Crimaudo, Calogero Terregino, Carolina Veneri, Giuseppina La Rosa, Elisabetta Suffredini
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5Nx) viruses, particularly clade 2.3.4.4b, pose substantial global health risks. We combined and piloted 2 duplex reverse transcription digital PCRs to discriminate between avian and seasonal influenza viruses in wastewater. This approach enabled rapid, strain-specific detection to reduce ambiguity in environmental surveillance for influenza viruses.

栖息地:禽流感 宿主 基因组学资源

Roost: Avian Influenza Host Genomics Resource
作者： Joseph G. Guhlin, Peter K. Dearden
摘要中文翻译高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 会导致家禽和野生动物出现大规模 死亡率,并使已经受到威胁的物种面临进一步的风险。了解 宿主 易感性的差异需要目前分散在出版物、监测 报告和基因组资源中的证据。我们推出 Roost,这是一个网络资源,汇集了 1,300 多种出版物和报告、监测 检测以及 2,000 多种鸟类 基因组 组件的目录。大语言模型辅助文献分析和比较基因组学将物种、基因和分子机制与其支持证据联系起来。大约 51% 检测到 HPAI 的物种并未出现在分析的语料库中。研究集中于家禽、模式生物和一小部分免疫途径。研究既没有追踪 死亡率 的大小,也没有追踪灭绝风险;有记录的死亡人数最多的物种和受威胁的 宿主 等物种在很大程度上仍未得到研究。Roost 为研究人员和保护从业者提供了这一证据以及 基因组 分析,将记录的疾病与 宿主 生物学联系起来,并强调测序和功能研究的优先事项。栖息地可在 http://josephguhlin.com/Roost 获取

Roost: Avian Influenza Host Genomics Resource

Authors: Joseph G. Guhlin, Peter K. Dearden
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) causes mass mortality in poultry and wildlife, and puts already threatened species at further risk. Understanding differences in host susceptibility requires evidence currently scattered across publications, surveillance reports and genomic resources. We present Roost, a web resource that brings together more than 1,300 publications and reports, surveillance detections, and a catalogue of over 2,000 avian genome assemblies. Large language model-assisted literature analysis and comparative genomics link species, genes and molecular mechanisms to their supporting evidence. Around 51% of species with HPAI detections are not represented in the analysed corpus. Research concentrates on poultry, model organisms, and a small set of immune pathways. Research tracks neither mortality magnitude nor extinction risk; species with the largest recorded die-offs and threatened hosts alike remain largely unstudied. Roost makes this evidence, along with genome analyses, accessible to researchers and conservation practitioners, connecting documented disease with host biology and highlighting priorities for sequencing and functional research. Roost is available at http://josephguhlin.com/Roost
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) causes mass mortality in poultry and wildlife, and puts already threatened species at further risk.
Design and population
Understanding differences in host susceptibility requires evidence currently scattered across publications, surveillance reports and genomic resources.
Core methods
Roost makes this evidence, along with genome analyses, accessible to researchers and conservation practitioners, connecting documented disease with host biology and highlighting priorities for sequencing and functional research.
Main results
Around 51% of species with HPAI detections are not represented in the analysed corpus.
Interpretation and novelty
Research concentrates on poultry, model organisms, and a small set of immune pathways.
Scientific and public-health significance
Roost is available at http://josephguhlin.com/Roost.
Limitations and evidence strength
Research tracks neither mortality magnitude nor extinction risk; species with the largest recorded die-offs and threatened hosts alike remain largely unstudied.

2024–2026 年埃及牲畜中 禽流感 病毒暴露的血清学证据

Serologic Evidence of Avian Influenza Virus Exposure in Livestock, Egypt, 2024–2026
作者： Mokhtar R. Gomaa, Rabeh El‐Shesheny, Ahmed El Taweel, Omnia Kutkat, Hager Emam, Basant Helal, Ayat Swelam, Mohamed GabAllah, Ahmed Kandeil, Pamela P. McKenzie
摘要中文翻译为了确定这些病毒是否蔓延到埃及的牲畜身上,特别是在混合物种环境中,我们在埃及北部进行了一项血清监测研究。我们的研究结果表明,禽流感 病毒对牛和其他牲畜的 跨物种溢出 不受地理限制,也不是由 H5N1 病毒的某些基因型引起的。此外,跨物种溢出 不仅限于牛;在我们的研究中测试的一些绵羊和山羊的甲型H9N2流感病毒血清呈阳性。我们用 χ2 或 Fisher 精确检验进行统计比较;我们认为 p<0.05 有统计学意义。免责声明:早期发布的文章不被视为最终版本。任何更改将在文章正式发布当月反映在在线版本中。第 32 卷第 12 期 - 2026 年 12 月 研究快报 2024-2026 年埃及牲畜中 禽流感 病毒暴露的血清学证据 建议引用本文 摘要 美国牛感染 A(H5N1) 流感病毒的 跨物种溢出 引发了全球关注。我们调查了地方性 禽流感 病毒是否可能蔓延到埃及的家畜中。我们在 2.5% 的测试动物中检测到 H9N2 流感抗体,在 2.9%–4.5% 的测试动物中检测到 H5N1 抗体。其他 监测 将阐明 宿主 感染动态。高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b 病毒在美国奶牛中的出现代表了流感病毒生态学的重大转变,标志着 H5N1 病毒 传播 在哺乳动物家畜物种中首次持续存在 (1)。2024 年初,美国几个州的奶牛中报道了基因型 B3.13;数据表明 跨物种溢出 事件从 野生鸟类 到牛,然后通过牛移动州际 传播 (1)。一个定义...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查地方性 禽流感 病毒是否正在扩散到埃及的家畜中,以解决人们对 禽流感 病毒 传播 可能传播到哺乳动物的担忧。
设计与对象
2024 年 10 月至 2026 年 1 月在埃及北部进行了一项血清监测研究,从 33 个养牛的小农场和后院收集了 444 份血清样本,其中一些农场和后院饲养着混合物种。该研究包括 3 个省,样本数量不同。
核心方法
根据世界卫生组织方案,使用血凝抑制 (HI) 和微量中和 (MN) 测定法检测血清样本中针对 H5N1 和 H9N2 禽流感 病毒的抗体。使用 χ2 或 Fisher 精确检验进行统计比较,以 p<0.05 作为显着性阈值。
主要结果
2.5% 的测试动物中检测到 H9N2 抗体,而 2.9%–4.5% 的动物中检测到 H5N1 抗体。所有 H9N2 阳性标本均来自同一省的 3 个地点 (p = 0.005),H5N1 抗体检出率因省而异(0%、1.3% 和 4.8%;p = 0.049)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,禽流感 病毒对牛和其他牲畜的 跨物种溢出 不受地理限制或仅限于 H5N1 病毒的特定基因型,这表明 传播 的潜力比之前观察到的更广泛。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果凸显了埃及和其他 禽流感 流行国家需要增强 禽流感 病毒的 监测,以更好地了解感染动态、传播 途径以及影响 跨物种溢出 牲畜的因素。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到血清学证据的限制,无法证实牛体内是否存在真正的感染或临床症状。然而,结果与欧洲报告的 跨物种溢出 事件一致,并为正在进行的 传播 提供了强有力的指标。

Serologic Evidence of Avian Influenza Virus Exposure in Livestock, Egypt, 2024–2026

Authors: Mokhtar R. Gomaa, Rabeh El‐Shesheny, Ahmed El Taweel, Omnia Kutkat, Hager Emam, Basant Helal, Ayat Swelam, Mohamed GabAllah, Ahmed Kandeil, Pamela P. McKenzie
Original abstract (English)
To determine whether those viruses are spilling over to livestock in Egypt, particularly in mixed-species settings, we conducted a serosurveillance study in northern Egypt. Our findings indicate that spillover of avian influenza viruses to cattle and other livestock is not geographically restricted or caused by certain genotypes of H5N1 virus. Furthermore, spillover is not restricted to cattle; some sheep and goats tested in our study were seropositive for influenza A(H9N2) virus. We performed statistical comparisons with χ2 or Fisher exact tests; we considered p<0.05 statistically significant. Disclaimer: Early release articles are not considered as final versions. Any changes will be reflected in the online version in the month the article is officially released. Volume 32, Number 12—December 2026 Research Letter Serologic Evidence of Avian Influenza Virus Exposure in Livestock, Egypt, 2024–2026 Suggested citation for this article Abstract Spillover of influenza A(H5N1) virus to cattle in the United States sparked global concern. We investigated whether endemic avian influenza viruses are potentially spilling over into domestic livestock in Egypt. We detected influenza H9N2 antibodies in 2.5% and H5N1 antibodies in 2.9%–4.5% of tested animals. Additional surveillance will elucidate host infection dynamics. The emergence of highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b virus in dairy cattle in the United States represented a major shift in influenza virus ecology, marking the first sustained transmission of H5N1 virus in a mammalian livestock species (1). Genotype B3.13 was reported in dairy cows in early 2024 in several US states; data indicated a spillover event from wild birds to cattle and then interstate transmission through cow movement (1). A definin...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether endemic avian influenza viruses are spilling over into domestic livestock in Egypt, addressing concerns about the potential for avian influenza virus transmission to mammals.
Design and population
A serosurveillance study was conducted in northern Egypt from October 2024 to January 2026, collecting 444 serum samples from 33 small farms and backyards with cattle, some of which housed mixed species. The study included 3 governorates with varying numbers of samples.
Core methods
Serum samples were tested for antibodies against H5N1 and H9N2 avian influenza viruses using hemagglutination inhibition (HI) and microneutralization (MN) assays following World Health Organization protocols. Statistical comparisons were made using χ2 or Fisher exact tests with p<0.05 as the threshold for significance.
Main results
H9N2 antibodies were detected in 2.5% of tested animals, while H5N1 antibodies were found in 2.9%–4.5%. All H9N2-positive specimens were from 3 locations in the same governorate (p = 0.005), and H5N1 antibody detection rates varied significantly by governorate (0%, 1.3%, and 4.8%; p = 0.049).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that spillover of avian influenza viruses to cattle and other livestock is not geographically restricted or limited to specific genotypes of H5N1 virus, indicating a broader potential for transmission than previously observed.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the need for enhanced surveillance of avian influenza viruses in Egypt and other countries with endemic avian influenza, to better understand infection dynamics, transmission routes, and factors influencing spillover to livestock.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its serologic evidence, which cannot confirm true infection or the presence of clinical signs in cattle. However, the results are consistent with spillover events reported in Europe and provide a strong indicator of ongoing transmission.

商业家禽的环境生物监测 超越临床症状:商业家禽生产中持续环境生物监测的案例

ENVIRONMENTAL BIOSURVEILLANCE IN COMMERCIAL POULTRY Beyond clinical signs: the case for continuous environmental biosurveillance in commercial poultry production
作者： Cassandra L. Ettinger, Natalie J. Urie, Elena G. Olson, Casey E. McGinley, Michael B. Rhys, Steven C. Ricke, Jake Byrnes
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了商业家禽生产中改进疾病检测的需求,强调了 高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) H5N1 等传染病所构成的威胁,该疾病自 2022 年以来已造成重大经济损失和禽类 死亡率。
设计与对象
该研究回顾了目前鸡群健康监测的做法,并评估了商业家禽环境中持续环境生物监测的潜力,重点关注选择鸡只进行采样和识别大部分无症状鸡群中病原体的挑战。
核心方法
作者讨论了环境采样技术,例如拖拭子、垃圾抓取样本、靴袜拭子、空气样本和水样,以及用于在没有事先确定病原体的情况下表征微生物多样性的多重 PCR 和宏基因组学方法。
主要结果
该研究报告称,商业家禽养殖中的疾病检测通常是反应性的,依赖于临床症状、死亡率 和产量下降,这会延迟 诊断 并导致过早做出 死亡率 决策。
解释与创新
作者解释说,环境生物监测为应对这些挑战提供了一种有前途的方法,但他们指出,在商业环境中常规实施还需要开展大量的技术和操作工作。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,采取主动的环境生物监测可以加强对 禽流感 和其他病原体的早期检测,支持在家禽生产中采取更有效的疾病预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于对当前实践的回顾,并且不提供原始数据,但它为商业家禽经营中转向主动疾病管理提供了有科学依据的理由。

ENVIRONMENTAL BIOSURVEILLANCE IN COMMERCIAL POULTRY Beyond clinical signs: the case for continuous environmental biosurveillance in commercial poultry production

Authors: Cassandra L. Ettinger, Natalie J. Urie, Elena G. Olson, Casey E. McGinley, Michael B. Rhys, Steven C. Ricke, Jake Byrnes
Original abstract (English)
Commercial poultry production in the United States is threatened by infectious disease, with highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 alone responsible for the destruction of more than 206 million birds and more than $1.4 billion in federal outbreak response and indemnity costs since 2022. Despite the scale of these losses, disease detection in commercial operations generally remains reactive, relying on the recognition of clinical signs, increased mortality, and production declines before diagnostic testing is initiated. This delay in diagnosis also creates an unnecessary urgency to begin treatment, leading some operators to apply blanket treatments before diagnostic results have been returned. Transitioning to proactive biosurveillance presents a promising alternative but introduces two new fundamental challenges: which individual birds should be selected for sampling from largely asymptomatic flocks, and which pathogens should be targeted for testing? Environmental sampling approaches—including drag swabs, litter grab samples, boot sock swabs, air samples, and water samples —offer one solution to the first challenge by aggregating pathogen shedding signals from an entire production unit into a single composite sample. While multiplexed PCR and metagenomics methods offer target-agnostic approaches to the second challenge by simultaneously characterizing some or all of the diversity of microbial taxa present in a sample without prior specification of which organisms may be causal. The objective of this review is to present (1) current flock health monitoring practices, (2) the promise and limitations of continuous environmental surveillance, (3) the range of environmental sample types available for poultry barns, and (4) how emerging multi-target testing approaches may address the lack of symptomatic guidance for what pathogens should be assessed. Together, this information represents a scientifically grounded path toward shifting commercial flock health management from reactive disease control to proactive prevention, though significant technical and operational work remains before routine commercial deployment is feasible.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for improved disease detection in commercial poultry production, highlighting the threat posed by infectious diseases such as highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1, which has caused significant economic losses and bird mortality since 2022.
Design and population
The study is a review of current practices in flock health monitoring and evaluates the potential of continuous environmental biosurveillance in commercial poultry settings, focusing on the challenges of selecting birds for sampling and identifying pathogens in largely asymptomatic flocks.
Core methods
The authors discuss environmental sampling techniques, such as drag swabs, litter grab samples, boot sock swabs, air samples, and water samples, as well as multiplexed PCR and metagenomics methods for characterizing microbial diversity without prior pathogen specification.
Main results
The study reports that disease detection in commercial poultry operations is typically reactive, relying on clinical signs, mortality, and production declines, which delays diagnosis and leads to premature treatment decisions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that environmental biosurveillance offers a promising approach to address these challenges, though they note that significant technical and operational work is needed for its routine implementation in commercial settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that adopting proactive environmental biosurveillance could enhance early detection of avian influenza and other pathogens, supporting more effective disease prevention strategies in poultry production.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a review of current practices and does not present original data, but it provides a scientifically grounded rationale for transitioning to proactive disease management in commercial poultry operations.
