禽流感 的野鸭超级脱落者表现出独特的泄殖腔微生物丰度分布

Mallard super-shedders of avian influenza exhibit distinct cloacal microbial abundance profiles
作者： Nelson Ruth, Migun Shakya, Cari D. Lewis, Cally E. Erickson, Amanda Dolinski, Mark Jankowski, Jeanne M. Fair, Jennifer C. Owen, Andrew W. Bartlow
摘要中文翻译摘要 在许多传染病系统中,宿主 的一小部分占 传播 的不成比例的份额,但这种异质性的生物学基础仍然没有得到很好的解决。在 禽流感 中,野鸭在病毒脱落方面表现出巨大差异,这增加了只有一小部分个体对环境污染和进一步传播做出重大贡献的可能性。为了检查 宿主 微生物组结构是否与这种变异相关,我们通过实验用低致病性 禽流感 病毒感染野生圈养野鸭。我们使用鸟枪法宏基因组测序结合基于读取的分类学分析和共组装宏基因组组装 基因组 (MAG) 重建,对对照、低脱落和高脱落鸟类的泄殖腔微生物组进行了表征。尽管受感染和未受感染的鸟类在物种存在与不存在方面高度相似,但基于丰度的分析显示,共享类群存在明显差异,特别是相对于对照组而言,高脱落鸟类。差异丰富的类群以属于包括 宿主 相关机会主义者在内的群体的类群为主,这与组成不平衡而不是完全的群落重组一致。假单胞菌门,特别是肠杆菌科和莫拉菌科,有很好的代表性。总之,这些发现表明,禽流感 感染与泄殖腔细菌类群丰度的变化有关,而不是物种成员的广泛变化,并且微生物组结构与关键野生动物 储存宿主 脱落的异质性有关。重要性 一小部分受感染的 宿主 通常导致病原体脱落的比例不成比例,但这种变异背后的生物学因素仍然知之甚少。在实验感染的野鸭(储存宿主 禽流感 A 病毒的一种重要野生动物)中,我们发现大量病毒脱落与泄殖腔细菌类群丰度的变化有关,而不是与微生物物种成员的广泛变化有关。这些结果表明,宿主 相关的微生物组结构可能与脱落异质性有关,并有助于解释为什么某些个体对环境污染和 传播 的影响更大。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 宿主 微生物组结构是否与 禽流感 中观察到的病毒脱落变化相关,因为 宿主 的一小部分占 传播 的不成比例的份额。
设计与对象
该研究涉及用低致病性 禽流感 病毒实验性感染野生和圈养野鸭,以检查关键野生动物 储存宿主 中 禽流感 A 病毒的脱落变异。
核心方法
使用鸟枪法宏基因组测序、基于读数的分类学分析和宏基因组组装 基因组 (MAG) 重建来表征对照、低脱落和高脱落鸟类的泄殖腔微生物组。
主要结果
基于丰度的分析揭示了共享细菌类群的明显差异,特别是与对照组相比,高脱落鸟类,而受感染和未受感染的鸟类在物种存在与不存在方面高度相似。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,禽流感 感染与泄殖腔细菌类群丰度的变化有关,而不是与微生物物种成员的广泛变化有关,这凸显了微生物组结构与脱落异质性之间的潜在关联。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可以为了解和减轻环境污染以及传播 A病毒的传播提供信息,特别是在主要野生动物储存宿主(例如野鸭)中。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于实验感染的野鸭,证据表明,微生物组的变化可能是导致脱落变异的一个促成因素,而不是一个决定性原因,具有中等的置信度评估。

Mallard super-shedders of avian influenza exhibit distinct cloacal microbial abundance profiles

Authors: Nelson Ruth, Migun Shakya, Cari D. Lewis, Cally E. Erickson, Amanda Dolinski, Mark Jankowski, Jeanne M. Fair, Jennifer C. Owen, Andrew W. Bartlow
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT In many infectious disease systems, a small fraction of hosts accounts for a disproportionate share of transmission, but the biological basis of this heterogeneity remains poorly resolved. In avian influenza, mallards show substantial variation in viral shedding, raising the possibility that only a subset of individuals contributes strongly to environmental contamination and onward spread. To examine whether host microbiome structure is associated with this variation, we experimentally infected wild, captive mallards with low-pathogenic avian influenza virus. We characterized cloacal microbiomes across control, low-shedding, and high-shedding birds using shotgun metagenomic sequencing combined with read-based taxonomic profiling and co-assembled metagenome-assembled genome (MAG) reconstruction. Although infected and uninfected birds were highly similar in species presence-absence, abundance-based analyses showed clear differences in shared taxa, particularly in high-shedding birds relative to controls. Differentially abundant taxa were dominated by taxa that belonged to groups that include host-associated opportunists, consistent with compositional imbalance rather than complete community restructuring. The phylum Pseudomonadota, especially the families Enterobacteriaceae and Moraxellaceae, is well represented. Together, these findings suggest that avian influenza infection is associated with shifts in the abundance of cloacal bacterial taxa rather than broad changes in species membership and that microbiome structure is associated with heterogeneity in shedding in a key wildlife reservoir. IMPORTANCE A small fraction of infected hosts often accounts for a disproportionate share of pathogen shedding, but the biological factors underlying this variation remain poorly understood. In experimentally infected mallards, a key wildlife reservoir for avian influenza A viruses, we found that high viral shedding was associated with shifts in the abundance of cloacal bacterial taxa rather than broad changes in microbial species membership. These results suggest that host-associated microbiome structure may be linked to shedding heterogeneity and could help explain why some individuals contribute more strongly to environmental contamination and onward transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether host microbiome structure is associated with variation in viral shedding observed in avian influenza, as a small fraction of hosts accounts for a disproportionate share of transmission.
Design and population
The study involved experimentally infecting wild and captive mallards with low-pathogenic avian influenza virus to examine shedding variation in a key wildlife reservoir for avian influenza A viruses.
Core methods
Cloacal microbiomes of control, low-shedding, and high-shedding birds were characterized using shotgun metagenomic sequencing, read-based taxonomic profiling, and metagenome-assembled genome (MAG) reconstruction.
Main results
Abundance-based analyses revealed clear differences in shared bacterial taxa, particularly in high-shedding birds compared to controls, while infected and uninfected birds were highly similar in species presence-absence.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that avian influenza infection is linked to shifts in the abundance of cloacal bacterial taxa rather than broad changes in microbial species membership, highlighting a potential association between microbiome structure and shedding heterogeneity.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results could inform strategies for understanding and mitigating environmental contamination and onward transmission by avian influenza A viruses, particularly in key wildlife reservoirs like mallards.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to experimentally infected mallards, and the evidence suggests that microbiome shifts may be a contributing factor rather than a definitive cause of shedding variation, with a moderate confidence assessment.

DNA 三棱柱设计的多色化学发光胶囊,用于核酸生物标志物的多重视觉检测。

DNA triangular prism-engineered multicolor chemiluminescence capsules for multiplexed visual detection of nucleic acid biomarkers.
作者： Xueyun Lu, Xiaohe Huo, Yafei Tian, Cuisong Zhou
摘要中文翻译化学发光(CL)胶囊具有持久发射和易于功能化的特点,在核酸生物标志物的多重分析中具有巨大的潜力。在此,我们报告了一种利用 DNA 三棱柱 (TP) 支架在多色 CL 胶囊上空间设计三价催化发夹组装 (CHA) 扩增的策略,用于 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) DNA 生物标志物的多重视觉检测。通过单独共封装鲁米诺、鲁米诺与香豆素 6 (C6)、鲁米诺与 C6 和尼罗红 (NR) 共封装,制备了三种多色 CL 胶囊,分别表示为 LUM@TP-3H2、C6/LUM@TP-3H4 和 NR/C6/LUM@TP-3H6。TP-3DNAzyme 的过氧化物酶样活性比溶液中的游离 DNAzyme 高约 1.6 倍,并且 TP 功能化胶囊比直接锚定 CHA 发夹的胶囊显示出更强的 CL 信号和更高的 CL 扩增效率。在目标触发 CHA 反应和添加 H2O2 后,这些 TP 功能化胶囊通过化学发光共振能量转移 (CRET) 分别产生蓝光、绿光和红光,并发出超过 1200 秒的强烈而持久的 CL 发射。基于胶囊的 CL 成像测定是用普通智能手机记录的,对 H1N1、H5N1 和 H7N9 的线性范围为 10-1000 pM,检测限分别为 0.57 pM、0.82 pM 和 1.97 pM,在 100 倍稀释的人血清中回收率为 97.41-104.87%。总而言之,这项工作通过使用 TP 设计的多色胶囊和三价 CHA 扩增,为基于智能手机的多重视觉检测提供了一个多功能的 CRET 平台。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种新的方法,利用化学发光 (CL) 胶囊和 DNA 三棱柱 (TP) 支架的独特特性,对核酸生物标志物进行多重视觉检测,特别是 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) DNA。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 DNA 三棱柱支架制造三种彩色 CL 胶囊,并测试其检测 AIV DNA 生物标志物的性能,包括 H5N1 和 H7N9 等特定亚型。
核心方法
研究人员使用具有三价催化发夹组装 (CHA) 放大功能的 TP 工程多色胶囊设计了一种 CL 成像测定,其中通过靶标触发反应的化学发光共振能量转移 (CRET) 产生 CL 信号。
主要结果
与直接锚定 CHA 发夹的胶囊相比,TP 功能化胶囊表现出更强的 CL 信号和更高的扩增效率,对 H1N1、H5N1 和 H7N9 的检测限分别为 0.57 pM、0.82 pM 和 1.97 pM。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,TP 设计的多色 CL 胶囊为基于智能手机的 AIV DNA 生物标志物多重视觉检测提供了一个多功能平台,提高了诊断方法的灵敏度和特异性。
科研与公卫意义
该方法可以通过使用智能手机快速、直观、灵敏地检测多种病毒亚型来改进 禽流感 A 病毒的 监测 和 诊断,特别是在资源有限的环境中。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的报告,由于缺乏详细的实验数据和现实临床或现场环境中的验证,证据强度中等。

DNA triangular prism-engineered multicolor chemiluminescence capsules for multiplexed visual detection of nucleic acid biomarkers.

Authors: Xueyun Lu, Xiaohe Huo, Yafei Tian, Cuisong Zhou
Original abstract (English)
Chemiluminescence (CL) capsules, featuring long-lasting emission and facile functionalization, hold great potential for multiplexed analysis of nucleic acid biomarkers. Herein, we report a strategy that utilizes DNA triangular prism (TP) scaffolds to spatially engineer trivalent catalytic hairpin assembly (CHA) amplification on multicolor CL capsules for the multiplexed visual detection of avian influenza virus (AIV) DNA biomarkers. Three multicolor CL capsules are fabricated by co-encapsulating luminol alone, luminol with coumarin 6 (C6), and luminol with both C6 and Nile red (NR), denoted as LUM@TP-3H2, C6/LUM@TP-3H4, and NR/C6/LUM@TP-3H6. TP-3DNAzyme exhibits approximately 1.6-fold higher peroxidase-like activity than free DNAzyme in solution, and TP-functionalized capsules show stronger CL signals and higher CL amplification efficiency than capsules with directly anchored CHA hairpins. Upon target-triggered CHA reaction and H2O2 addition, these TP-functionalized capsules generate blue, green, and red light, respectively, through chemiluminescence resonance energy transfer (CRET), along with an intense and long-lasting CL emission over 1200 s. The capsule-based CL imaging assay is recorded with a common smartphone, exhibiting a linear range of 10-1000 pM and detection limits of 0.57 pM, 0.82 pM, and 1.97 pM for H1N1, H5N1, and H7N9, respectively, with recoveries of 97.41-104.87% in 100-fold diluted human serum. Altogether, this work provides a versatile CRET platform by using TP-engineered multicolor capsules with trivalent CHA amplification for smartphone-based multiplexed visual detection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a novel method for the multiplexed visual detection of nucleic acid biomarkers, particularly for avian influenza virus (AIV) DNA, leveraging the unique properties of chemiluminescence (CL) capsules and DNA triangular prism (TP) scaffolds.
Design and population
The study involved the fabrication of three multicolor CL capsules using DNA triangular prism scaffolds and tested their performance for the detection of AIV DNA biomarkers, including specific subtypes such as H5N1 and H7N9.
Core methods
The researchers designed a CL imaging assay using TP-engineered multicolor capsules with trivalent catalytic hairpin assembly (CHA) amplification, where CL signals were generated through chemiluminescence resonance energy transfer (CRET) upon target-triggered reactions.
Main results
TP-functionalized capsules demonstrated stronger CL signals and higher amplification efficiency compared to capsules with directly anchored CHA hairpins, with detection limits of 0.57 pM, 0.82 pM, and 1.97 pM for H1N1, H5N1, and H7N9, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the TP-engineered multicolor CL capsules offer a versatile platform for smartphone-based multiplexed visual detection of AIV DNA biomarkers, enhancing the sensitivity and specificity of diagnostic methods.
Scientific and public-health significance
This method could improve the surveillance and diagnosis of avian influenza A viruses, particularly in resource-limited settings, by enabling rapid, visual, and sensitive detection of multiple viral subtypes using a smartphone.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level reporting, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed experimental data and validation in real-world clinical or field settings.

小规模家禽养殖户对 禽流感 生物安全实践的认识、态度和采用

Awareness, attitude and adoption of avian influenza biosecurity practices among smallholder poultry farmers
作者： Akanksha Singh, Hari R, Devesh Singh, Mandavi Kaushik, Akshay Jain
摘要中文翻译禽流感 仍然是对家禽生产和农民生计的重要威胁,特别是在生物安全实践往往受到限制的小农系统中。本研究评估了农民对推荐的 禽流感 生物安全实践的认识、态度和采用情况,并研究了它们与选定的社会经济特征的关联。该研究于 2019-20 年在覆盖中央邦贾巴尔普尔区所有七个街区的 14 个村庄进行。通过多阶段抽样,选择了总共 140 家饲养少于 100 只家禽的小农户,并按照结构化时间表进行了访谈。使用包含 25 个项目的表来评估意识,使用包含 14 个项目的量表来评估态度,使用包含 5 个生物安全领域的 43 个项目的表来评估采用度。使用频率、百分比、相关性和多元回归分析数据。调查结果显示,60.71%的农户知晓率较低,而只有12.86%的农户知晓率较高。54.29% 的农民态度得分非常低。总体而言,大多数农民 (82.86%) 对推荐的生物安全措施的采用率非常低,而只有 1.42% 的采用率较高。意识和态度与采用呈正相关。研究结果强调需要实用培训、可获得的兽医推广服务和当地相关的沟通,以帮助农民改善农场一级的生物安全实践。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽流感 仍然是对家禽生产和农民生计的重要威胁,特别是在生物安全实践往往受到限制的小农系统中。
设计与对象
本研究评估了农民对推荐的 禽流感 生物安全实践的认识、态度和采用情况,并研究了它们与选定的社会经济特征的关联。
核心方法
使用包含 25 个项目的表来评估意识,使用包含 14 个项目的量表来评估态度,使用包含 5 个生物安全领域的 43 个项目的表来评估采用度。使用频率、百分比、相关性和多元回归分析数据。
主要结果
调查结果显示,60.71%的农户知晓率较低,而只有12.86%的农户知晓率较高。54.29% 的农民态度得分非常低。总体而言,大多数农民 (82.86%) 对推荐的生物安全措施的采用率非常低,而只有 1.42% 的采用率较高。

意识和态度与采用呈正相关。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要实用培训、可获得的兽医推广服务和当地相关的沟通,以帮助农民改善农场一级的生物安全实践。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Awareness, attitude and adoption of avian influenza biosecurity practices among smallholder poultry farmers

Authors: Akanksha Singh, Hari R, Devesh Singh, Mandavi Kaushik, Akshay Jain
Original abstract (English)
Avian influenza remains an important threat to poultry production and farmers’ livelihoods, particularly in smallholder systems where biosecurity practices are often limited. The present study assessed farmers’ awareness, attitude and adoption of recommended avian influenza biosecurity practices and examined their association with selected socioeconomic characteristics. The study was conducted during 2019-20 in 14 villages covering all seven blocks of Jabalpur district, Madhya Pradesh. A total of 140 smallholder poultry farmers keeping fewer than 100 birds were selected through multistage sampling and interviewed using structured schedules. Awareness was assessed using a 25-item schedule, attitude using an adopted 14-item scale, and adoption using a 43-item schedule covering five biosecurity domains. Data were analyzed using frequency, percentage, correlation and multiple regression. The findings showed that 60.71% of farmers had low awareness, while only 12.86% had high awareness. Very low attitude scores were observed among 54.29% of farmers. Overall, most farmers (82.86%) had very low adoption of the recommended biosecurity practices, while only 1.42% showed a high level of adoption. Awareness and attitude were positively associated with adoption. The findings highlight the need for practical training, accessible veterinary extension services and locally relevant communication to help farmers improve biosecurity practices at the farm level.
View research seven-element analysis

Avian influenza remains an important threat to poultry production and farmers’ livelihoods, particularly in smallholder systems where biosecurity practices are often limited.
Design and population
The present study assessed farmers’ awareness, attitude and adoption of recommended avian influenza biosecurity practices and examined their association with selected socioeconomic characteristics.
Core methods
Awareness was assessed using a 25-item schedule, attitude using an adopted 14-item scale, and adoption using a 43-item schedule covering five biosecurity domains. Data were analyzed using frequency, percentage, correlation and multiple regression.
Main results
The findings showed that 60.71% of farmers had low awareness, while only 12.86% had high awareness. Very low attitude scores were observed among 54.29% of farmers. Overall, most farmers (82.86%) had very low adoption of the recommended biosecurity practices, while only 1.42% showed a high level of adoption.
Interpretation and novelty
Awareness and attitude were positively associated with adoption.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for practical training, accessible veterinary extension services and locally relevant communication to help farmers improve biosecurity practices at the farm level.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

H5N5 病毒 传播 是由反复跨大西洋传入和高纬度海鸟中的 传播 驱动的

H5N5 virus transmission is driven by repeated cross-Atlantic introductions and transmission among high latitude seabirds
作者： Clara R. Malekshahi, Nicole St. Clair Knobloch, Alvin Crespo-Bellido, Alexa D. Simulynas, Stephanie Goya, Leigh K. Papalambros, William J. Fitzsimmons, Alexander L. Greninger, Adam S. Lauring, Jonathan A. Runstadler
摘要中文翻译2025 年 11 月,华盛顿州报告了全球首例 H5N5 感染病例。此前,这些病毒仅在大西洋附近的海鸟和滨鸟中检测到,这表明它们与主要的 H5N1 病毒株可能具有不同的生态学特征。使用 354 个全球 H5N5 基因组,我们发现 H5N5 病毒已被高纬度海鸟和滨鸟携带跨越大西洋至少 9 次,在秋季和春季引入更为频繁。我们重建了一条通过北极或亚北极地区跨大陆运动的合理路径,随后是人类 跨物种溢出 之前的一年神秘循环。这些数据表明,H5N5 病毒经历的洲际移动远比之前认识的要多,并且表现出与主要 H5N1 谱系根本不同的 宿主 和地理模式。独特的 宿主 生态、地理和运动,再加上感染人的能力,表明增强的 监测 可能是有必要的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 H5N5 病毒的 传播 动态,并解决有关其全球传播和生态模式的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究分析了全球 354 种 H5N5 基因组,以研究这些病毒的跨大西洋 传播 模式和 宿主 相互作用,重点关注高纬度海鸟和滨鸟。
核心方法
研究人员使用 354 种 H5N5 病毒的基因组数据来重建 传播 通路并评估跨大陆移动的频率,从而确定引入的季节性模式。
主要结果
2025 年 11 月,华盛顿州报告了全球首例 H5N5 感染病例,数据表明 H5N5 病毒已跨大西洋传播至少 9 次,主要发生在秋季和春季。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,与主要的 H5N1 谱系相比,H5N5 病毒经历了比之前认识的更多的洲际传播,具有独特的 宿主 和地理模式。
科研与公卫意义
独特的 宿主 生态、地理运动和人类 跨物种溢出 的潜力表明需要增强 H5N5 病毒的 监测 以更好地了解和减轻其传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于基因组数据,不包括直接实验验证,这可能会限制重建的 传播 通路的确定性。然而,考虑到全面的数据集,证据是强有力的。

H5N5 virus transmission is driven by repeated cross-Atlantic introductions and transmission among high latitude seabirds

Authors: Clara R. Malekshahi, Nicole St. Clair Knobloch, Alvin Crespo-Bellido, Alexa D. Simulynas, Stephanie Goya, Leigh K. Papalambros, William J. Fitzsimmons, Alexander L. Greninger, Adam S. Lauring, Jonathan A. Runstadler
Original abstract (English)
In November 2025, the first global H5N5 infection was reported in a person in Washington State. Previously, these viruses had only been detected in seabirds and shorebirds near the Atlantic Ocean, suggesting potentially distinct ecology from dominant H5N1 strains. Using 354 global H5N5 genomes, we show that H5N5 viruses have been carried by high latitude seabirds and shorebirds across the Atlantic at least 9 times, with introductions more frequent in the fall and spring. We reconstruct a plausible path of cross-continental movement via Arctic or sub-Arctic regions, followed by a year of cryptic circulation prior to human spillover. These data show that H5N5 viruses have undergone far more intercontinental movements than previously recognized, and exhibit host and geographic patterns that are fundamentally distinct from dominant H5N1 lineages. The distinct host ecology, geography, and movement, paired with the capacity to infect a person, suggest that enhanced surveillance may be warranted.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the transmission dynamics of H5N5 viruses and address the knowledge gap regarding their global spread and ecological patterns.
Design and population
The study analyzed 354 global H5N5 genomes to examine the cross-Atlantic transmission patterns and host interactions of these viruses, focusing on high latitude seabirds and shorebirds.
Core methods
The researchers used genomic data from 354 H5N5 viruses to reconstruct transmission pathways and assess the frequency of cross-continental movements, identifying seasonal patterns of introduction.
Main results
The first global H5N5 infection in a person was reported in November 2025 in Washington State, and the data indicate that H5N5 viruses have been introduced across the Atlantic at least 9 times, predominantly in the fall and spring.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal that H5N5 viruses have undergone more intercontinental movements than previously recognized, with distinct host and geographic patterns compared to dominant H5N1 lineages.
Scientific and public-health significance
The distinct host ecology, geographic movement, and potential for human spillover suggest the need for enhanced surveillance of H5N5 viruses to better understand and mitigate their spread.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on genomic data and does not include direct experimental validation, which may limit the certainty of the reconstructed transmission pathways. However, the evidence is strong given the comprehensive dataset.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

沉默的旅行者:候鸟及其蜱虫将新出现的病毒引入瑞士的潜力

Silent travelers: the potential of migratory birds and their ticks to introduce emerging viruses into Switzerland
作者： Corinne Manuela Gerber, Giulia Zezza, Valeria Guidi, Michele Wyler, Claudia Bachofen, Jakub Kubacki
摘要中文翻译摘要 瑞士 宿主 有 400 多种鸟类,其中许多是候鸟。通过不同的运动模式,从短距离到几乎全球性的迁徙,这些鸟类可以引入人畜共患病毒,例如西尼罗河病毒(WNV)或禽流感病毒(AIV),以及未知和潜在新出现的病毒。此外,它们还促进了受感染的体外寄生虫(尤其是蜱虫)在全球的传播,从而带来了引入新型蜱传疾病的风险。为了评估这种风险,我们在 2023 年的七个月内对提契诺州(瑞士)的 Bolle di Magadino 自然保护区和 Piano di Magadino 地区的 42 种鸟类(39 种候鸟,3 种留鸟)进行了采样。该地区气候过渡,是候鸟的重要休息和繁殖地。总共收集了 496 份口咽泄殖腔拭子、164 份血清和 22 只相关蜱。虽然拭子的 RT-qPCR 没有检测到活跃的 WNV 或 AIV 感染,但 ELISA 测试在 164 份血清中的 15 份中发现了正黄病毒抗体,表明之前曾接触过。宏基因组下一代测序揭示了跨越 33 个科的病毒景观(其中 29 个科是在鸟类中发现的)。在该病毒组中,DNA病毒(主要是圆环病毒科、基因病毒科和细小病毒科)在丰度上占主导地位,但表现出较低的多样性。相比之下,高度多样化的RNA病毒组主要反映了鸟类的饮食习惯和自然保护区的当地栖息地。值得注意的是,我们报告在瑞士首次检测到伯乐蜱病毒 4 (BoTV4),该病毒在与长途迁徙有关的几种蜱 (Rhipicephalus turanicus) 中被发现。这种新建立的瘟病毒科成员已经在亚洲、非洲和欧洲的部分地区定居。这项研究提供了瑞士第一个活跃的 野生鸟类 病毒组 监测。我们的研究结果为早期检测新出现的病毒建立了一个框架,并强调了候鸟作为蜱虫和新型蜱传病毒传播 媒介 的作用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估新出现的病毒通过候鸟及其相关蜱传入瑞士的风险。瑞士宿主有超过400种鸟类,其中许多是候鸟,这些鸟类可以引入人畜共患病毒,例如西尼罗河病毒(WNV)或禽流感病毒(AIV),以及未知和潜在新出现的病毒。
设计与对象
该研究于2023年在瑞士提契诺州的Bolle di Magadino自然保护区和Piano di Magadino地区进行了七个月。研究对42种鸟类(其中39种为候鸟,3种为留鸟)进行了采样,以评估通过候鸟迁徙模式及其相关外寄生虫引入病毒的潜力。
核心方法
RT-qPCR 用于检测口咽泄殖腔拭子是否存在活动性 WNV 或 AIV 感染,而 ELISA 则用于鉴定血清中的正黄病毒抗体。采用宏基因组下一代测序来分析 33 个科的病毒状况,重点是检测新型蜱传病毒。
主要结果
拭子的 RT-qPCR 未检测到活跃的 WNV 或 AIV 感染。ELISA 检测在 164 份血清中的 15 份中发现了正黄病毒抗体,表明之前曾接触过该病毒。宏基因组测序揭示了涵盖 33 个科的病毒景观,其中 DNA 病毒主要是圆环病毒科、基因病毒科和细小病毒科,数量最多,但多样性较低。
解释与创新
研究结果为早期检测新出现的病毒建立了一个框架,并强调了候鸟作为蜱虫和新型蜱传病毒传播 媒介 的作用。值得注意的是,该研究报告在瑞士首次检测到伯乐蜱病毒 4 (BoTV4),该病毒在几种与长途迁徙有关的蜱 (Rhipicephalus turanicus) 中被发现。
科研与公卫意义
这项研究提供了关于候鸟将新兴病毒引入瑞士的可能性的重要见解,强调了 野生鸟类 病毒组中活跃的 监测 的必要性,以监测人畜共患病威胁并为公共卫生做好准备提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象分析,这可能会限制解释的深度。证据的强度中等,因为它依赖于采样和测序的初步结果,没有详细的后续或验证数据。

Silent travelers: the potential of migratory birds and their ticks to introduce emerging viruses into Switzerland

Authors: Corinne Manuela Gerber, Giulia Zezza, Valeria Guidi, Michele Wyler, Claudia Bachofen, Jakub Kubacki
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Switzerland hosts over 400 bird species, many of which are migratory. Through diverse movement patterns, ranging from short distances to almost global migrations, these birds can introduce zoonotic viruses, such as West Nile virus (WNV) or avian influenza virus (AIV), as well as unknown and potentially emerging viruses. Furthermore, they facilitate the global dispersal of infected ectoparasites, particularly ticks, risking the introduction of novel tick-borne diseases. To assess this risk, we sampled 42 bird species (39 migratory, 3 resident) over seven months in 2023 at the Bolle di Magadino nature reserve and the Piano di Magadino area in the canton Ticino (Switzerland). This region, because of its transitional climate, serves as an important resting and breeding ground for migratory birds. In total, 496 oropharyngeal-cloacal swabs, 164 sera, and 22 associated ticks were collected. While RT-qPCR of the swabs detected no active WNV or AIV infections, ELISA testing identified Orthoflavivirus antibodies in 15 of 164 sera, indicating prior exposure. Metagenomic next-generation sequencing revealed a viral landscape spanning 33 families (29 of which were identified in birds). Within this virome, DNA viruses, predominantly Circoviridae, Genomoviridae, and Parvoviridae , dominated in abundance but exhibited low diversity. In contrast, the highly diverse RNA virome primarily reflected the birds’ dietary habits and the local habitat at the nature reserve. Notably, we report the first detection of Bole tick virus 4 (BoTV4) in Switzerland, identified in several ticks ( Rhipicephalus turanicus ) associated with long-distance migrants. This member of the newly established Pestiviridae family is already established in parts of Asia, Africa, and Europe. This study provides the first active surveillance of the wild bird virome in Switzerland. Our findings establish a framework for the early detection of emerging viruses and underscore the role of migratory birds as dispersal vectors for ticks and novel tick-borne viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the risk of emerging viruses being introduced into Switzerland through migratory birds and their associated ticks. Switzerland hosts over 400 bird species, many of which are migratory, and these birds can introduce zoonotic viruses, such as West Nile virus (WNV) or avian influenza virus (AIV), as well as unknown and potentially emerging viruses.
Design and population
The study was conducted over seven months in 2023 at the Bolle di Magadino nature reserve and the Piano di Magadino area in the canton Ticino (Switzerland). It sampled 42 bird species (39 migratory, 3 resident) to evaluate the potential for virus introduction through migratory patterns and associated ectoparasites.
Core methods
RT-qPCR was used to test oropharyngeal-cloacal swabs for active WNV or AIV infections, while ELISA identified Orthoflavivirus antibodies in sera. Metagenomic next-generation sequencing was employed to analyze the viral landscape across 33 families, with a focus on detecting novel tick-borne viruses.
Main results
RT-qPCR of the swabs detected no active WNV or AIV infections. ELISA testing identified Orthoflavivirus antibodies in 15 of 164 sera, indicating prior exposure. Metagenomic sequencing revealed a viral landscape spanning 33 families, with DNA viruses predominantly Circoviridae, Genomoviridae, and Parvoviridae dominating in abundance but exhibiting low diversity.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings establish a framework for the early detection of emerging viruses and highlight the role of migratory birds as dispersal vectors for ticks and novel tick-borne viruses. Notably, the study reports the first detection of Bole tick virus 4 (BoTV4) in Switzerland, identified in several ticks (Rhipicephalus turanicus) associated with long-distance migrants.
Scientific and public-health significance
This study provides critical insights into the potential for migratory birds to introduce emerging viruses into Switzerland, emphasizing the need for active surveillance of the wild bird virome to monitor zoonotic threats and inform public health preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, which may restrict the depth of interpretation. The evidence is moderate in strength, as it relies on initial findings from sampling and sequencing without detailed follow-up or confirmatory data.

有缺陷的病毒 基因组 揭示了 I 型干扰素动力学的历史依赖性自我调节

Defective Viral Genomes Reveal History-dependent Self-regulation of Type I Interferon Dynamics
作者： Yi-Mei Li, Carla Lourens, Bjarke Frost Nielsen, Simon A. Levin, Alan S. Perelson, Bryan Thomas Grenfell, Aartjan J.W. te Velthuis
摘要中文翻译摘要 I 型干扰素 (IFN) 有助于清除病毒感染,但 IFN 水平可能会下降,从而重置 抗病毒药物 状态并避免先天免疫失调。这种失调可能发生在感染高致病性和大流行性 RNA 病毒(例如 H5N1 甲型流感病毒或 SARS-CoV-2)后。尽管这些病毒诱导出强大的先天 免疫应答,但大流行病毒仍能成功复制和传播,这表明它们可以规避 IFN 的影响。当 宿主 细胞检测到病毒 RNA,尤其是有缺陷的病毒 基因组 (DVG) 时,会触发先天免疫激活。然后,强烈的 IFN 反应(例如对 DVG 或高免疫原性野生型感染)必须通过涉及 IFN 受体的负反馈而减弱。DVG 如何调节这种反馈仍然知之甚少。为了量化IFN的正向和负向调节,我们首先结合不同剂量的病毒RNA和外源IFN进行体外实验,并测量随后的IFN表达。接下来,我们开发了一种体内贝叶斯计算模型,该模型可以捕获流感病毒 DVG 对 IFN 正反馈和负反馈的影响。还考虑了 DVG 和病毒之间的竞争。最后,我们根据已发表的体外和体内流感病毒感染数据校准了该模型。我们的模型表明,宿主 细胞的累积 IFN 暴露,而不是瞬时 IFN 水平,决定了进一步的 IFN 诱导;这赋予 DVG 双重作用:除了触发 IFN 诱导之外,它们还可以通过负反馈降低 IFN 诱导。我们的模型进一步预测这些效应在局部很重要,塑造了反应的空间动态,从而抑制邻近未感染细胞中的先天 免疫应答。通过这个模型,我们可以解释两个重要的生物学观察结果:i)在组织培养和小鼠感染中,IFN 水平可能下降,而病毒和 DVG 水平继续上升;ii) 产生高水平免疫原性 RNA 分子的大流行病毒可以通过局部先天免疫抑制成功复制。总体而言,我们认为病毒群体内的异质性(包括 DVG 和其他非典型病毒 RNA)可以调节正反馈环和负反馈环并塑造感染结果。更广泛地说,我们的工作可能支持这些病毒 RNA 群体的治疗用途的研究。作者摘要 人类和动物必须在不引发免疫病理学的情况下对抗流感病毒和其他感染。检测到感染后,先天免疫系统会产生 I 型干扰素 (IFN),这是一种启动保护性反应的信号。然而,高水平的干扰素和延长的干扰素表达会导致组织损伤并使疾病恶化。负反馈是干扰素产生停止的一种途径,但其在病毒感染期间的激活仍知之甚少。一个核心问题是,为什么尽管有强烈的干扰素反应,病毒仍能继续复制和传播。我们在这里研究了为什么在流感病毒感染期间,干扰素水平会开始下降,而病毒水平继续上升。当病毒产生大量有缺陷的病毒 基因组 (DVG) 时,这种下降最为明显。这些有缺陷的 基因组 在病毒复制过程中自然出现,由许多 RNA 病毒产生,包括 SARS-CoV-2、埃博拉病毒和麻疹病毒。通过细胞培养实验、计算建模以及将我们的模型与细胞培养和小鼠数据进行拟合,我们发现有缺陷的 基因组 会导致 IFN 激活及其负反馈。实验和模型都支持感染过程中 IFN 积累抑制其自身产生的机制,并且这种抑制可以在强 DVG 刺激下更早开始。在地方层面,即使总体干扰素水平仍然很高,这也可能导致病毒继续传播。总体而言,我们的研究结果揭示了有缺陷的病毒 基因组 和 宿主 先天免疫系统之间的额外相互作用。他们还建议,必须仔细校准有缺陷的 基因组 的潜在治疗用途,以实现最佳的 抗病毒药物 反应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解病毒感染期间 I 型干扰素 (IFN) 动态如何受到调节,特别关注有缺陷的病毒 基因组 (DVG) 在调节 IFN 反应中的作用。背景强调,尽管先天 免疫应答 很强,但 IFN 水平仍可能下降,这对于避免免疫病理学至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究将体外实验与体内贝叶斯计算模型相结合,研究流感病毒 DVG 对 IFN 反馈的影响。它使用已发表的体外和体内流感病毒感染数据进行校准。
核心方法
研究人员用不同剂量的病毒RNA和外源干扰素进行了体外实验,以测量干扰素的表达。他们还开发了一个计算模型来模拟 DVG 对 IFN 反馈的影响,并使用现有的感染数据对其进行校准。
主要结果
该模型表明,累积的干扰素暴露,而不是瞬时水平,决定了进一步的干扰素诱导。DVG 被证明可以触发 IFN 激活,同时也有助于负反馈,从而抑制邻近细胞中先天的 免疫应答。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了 DVG 在激活和抑制 IFN 反应中的双重作用,为病毒如何逃避 免疫应答 提供了一种新机制。这解释了两个关键的观察结果:当病毒水平上升时,干扰素水平可能下降,大流行病毒可以通过局部免疫抑制进行复制。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 DVG 和 宿主 免疫系统之间复杂相互作用的见解,这可以为针对病毒 RNA 群体的治疗干预策略提供信息,以优化 抗病毒药物 反应。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于体外和体内数据,这可能会限制普遍性。然而,该模型是根据已发布的数据进行校准的,并且证据得到实验和计算方法的支持,使其具有中等的置信度。

Defective Viral Genomes Reveal History-dependent Self-regulation of Type I Interferon Dynamics

Authors: Yi-Mei Li, Carla Lourens, Bjarke Frost Nielsen, Simon A. Levin, Alan S. Perelson, Bryan Thomas Grenfell, Aartjan J.W. te Velthuis
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Type I interferons (IFN) help clear a viral infection, but IFN levels can then decline to reset the antiviral state and avoid innate immune dysregulation. Such dysregulation can occur following infection with highly pathogenic and pandemic RNA viruses, such as H5N1 influenza A viruses or SARS-CoV-2. In spite of the strong innate immune response that these viruses induce, pandemic viruses replicate and spread successfully, suggesting that they can circumvent the effects of IFN. Innate immune activation is triggered when host cells detect viral RNA, especially defective viral genomes (DVGs). A strong IFN response (e.g. to DVGs or a highly immunogenic wild-type infection) must then decline through negative feedback involving the IFN receptor. How DVGs modulate this feedback remains poorly understood. To quantify positive and negative IFN regulation, we first performed in vitro experiments combining different doses of viral RNA and exogenous IFN, and measured subsequent IFN expression. We next developed an in vivo Bayesian computational model that captures the impact of influenza virus DVGs on both positive and negative IFN feedback. Competition between DVGs and virus is also considered. Finally, we calibrated this model on published in vitro and in vivo influenza virus infection data. Our model suggests that the cumulative IFN exposure of a host cell, rather than the instantaneous IFN level, shapes further IFN induction; this gives DVGs a dual role: beyond triggering IFN induction, they can also lower it through negative feedback. Our model further predicts that these effects are important locally, shaping the spatial dynamics of the response, allowing suppression of innate immune responses in neighbouring, uninfected cells. Through this model, we can explain two important biological observations: i) IFN levels can decline while virus and DVG levels continue to rise in tissue culture and mouse infections; and ii) pandemic viruses that produce high levels of immunogenic RNA molecules can successfully replicate through local innate immune suppression. Overall, we propose that heterogeneity within viral populations (including DVGs and other noncanonical viral RNAs) can modulate positive and negative feedback loops and shape infection outcomes. More broadly, our work may support studies into the therapeutic use of these viral RNA populations. Author summary Humans and animals have to fight influenza virus and other infections without inducing immunopathology. After detection of an infection, the innate immune system produces type I interferon (IFN), a signal that starts a protective response. However, high levels of IFN and prolonged IFN expression can cause damage to tissues and worsen disease. Negative feedback is one route by which IFN production can stop, but its activation during viral infection remains poorly understood. A central question is why viruses can continue to replicate and spread despite a strong IFN response. We here investigated why IFN levels can begin to decline while virus levels continue to rise during influenza virus infection. This decline is most pronounced when the virus produces large amounts of defective viral genomes (DVGs). These defective genomes arise naturally during viral replication and are produced by many RNA viruses, including SARS-CoV-2, Ebola virus, and measles virus. Using cell-culture experiments, computational modelling, and fitting of our model to cell-culture and mouse data, we find that defective genomes contribute to both IFN activation and its negative feedback. Both the experiments and model support a mechanism in which IFN accumulation during infection suppresses its own production, and that this suppression can start earlier under strong DVG stimulation. At the local level, this can allow viral spread to continue even while overall IFN levels remain high. Overall, our findings reveal an additional level of interplay between defective viral genomes and the host innate immune system. They also suggest that the potential therapeutic use of defective genomes must be carefully calibrated to achieve an optimal antiviral response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how type I interferon (IFN) dynamics are regulated during viral infections, particularly focusing on the role of defective viral genomes (DVGs) in modulating IFN responses. The background highlights that IFN levels can decline despite strong innate immune responses, which is critical for avoiding immunopathology.
Design and population
The study combined in vitro experiments with an in vivo Bayesian computational model to investigate the impact of influenza virus DVGs on IFN feedback. It used published in vitro and in vivo influenza virus infection data for calibration.
Core methods
The researchers conducted in vitro experiments with varying doses of viral RNA and exogenous IFN to measure IFN expression. They also developed a computational model to simulate the effects of DVGs on IFN feedback and calibrated it using existing infection data.
Main results
The model suggests that cumulative IFN exposure, rather than instantaneous levels, shapes further IFN induction. DVGs are shown to trigger IFN activation while also contributing to negative feedback, which can suppress innate immune responses in neighboring cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal a dual role of DVGs in both activating and suppressing IFN responses, offering a novel mechanism for how viruses can evade immune responses. This explains two key observations: IFN levels can decline while virus levels rise, and pandemic viruses can replicate through local immune suppression.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the complex interplay between DVGs and the host immune system, which could inform strategies for therapeutic interventions targeting viral RNA populations to optimize antiviral responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on in vitro and in vivo data, which may limit generalizability. However, the model is calibrated on published data, and the evidence is supported by both experimental and computational approaches, giving it a moderate confidence level.

流感热稳定性

Influenza thermostability
作者： Jenna Schafers
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解热导致病毒失活的机制,特别是在甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 的背景下,该病毒历来导致显着的发病率和 死亡率。这一背景凸显了了解病毒在面对环境挑战时如何保持活力的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究重点考察了 bulk 溶液中的热稳定性,分析了在不同温度条件下 IAV 的失活情况。该研究是爱丁堡大学博士论文的一部分,发表于 2026-11-04。
核心方法
本研究采用热力学分析和HA构象变化的3态数学模型来探讨IAV失活机制。同时,还考察了HA的修饰,如氨基酸替换或切割位点的阻断,以评估它们对失活速率的影响。
主要结果
研究发现,IAV 感染力在 50°C 以下表现出一级指数衰减,表明存在非合作灭活机制。两个线性状态(高于和低于 40 °C)之间的阿伦尼乌斯行为表明,不同的主要失活途径因温度而分开。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,IAV 在 40-50 °C 时失活与蛋白质解折叠一致,特别是与病毒血凝素 (HA) 相关。这为 IAV 的温度依赖性稳定性及其对消毒方法的影响提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
了解热导致病毒灭活的机制可以提高消毒方案的功效并具有更广泛的应用,例如增强 疫苗 的稳定性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,这可能会限制分析的深度。然而,这些发现得到了热力学分析和数学模型的支持,为结论提供了中等程度的可信度。

Influenza thermostability

Authors: Jenna Schafers
Original abstract (English)
Even prior to the advent of germ theory in the 19th century, disease inflicted by viruses and the scale at which this could occur was recognised and feared, if not understood. Some viruses, such as influenza A virus (IAV), have been the cause of great morbidity and mortality throughout history. Their ability to transmit between hosts is key to their pandemic potential. This is not trivial, requiring the virus to surmount a veritable obstacle course of environmental insults before reaching safe harbour; a compatible host organism. Surprisingly, this area is relatively unstudied, with a dearth of clarity on how this occurs, and perhaps most importantly, the bounds of virus viability. Temperature is one of the key environmental factors of viral stability. Exploited as a means of pathogen eradication throughout history, likely predating any concept of micro-organisms, it remains in use today, particularly in the food and healthcare industry, e.g pasteurisation. Fortunately this process remains effective against IAV, reassuring when avian influenza spread to dairy cattle. Understanding the mechanism of viral inactivation due to heat would allow us to improve the efficacy of disinfection via this method and likely have other applications, for example in vaccine stability. This study is an effort to disentangle the complex environmental factors by focusing on thermostability in bulk solution. It found that IAV infectivity demonstrated first-order exponential decay below 50 °C, indicating a non-cooperative inactivation mechanism. Arrhenius behaviour between two linear regimes (above and below 40 °C) suggest there are different dominant inactivation pathways separated by temperature. The 40-50 °C regime was further elucidated in view of its implications on disinfection. Thermodynamic analysis suggests a process consistent with protein unfolding, particularly implicating the viral haemagglutinin (HA). Alternative inactivation mechanisms such as virion rupture, aggregation or failure of receptor binding were excluded. Modifications to HA, such as amino-acid substitution or prevention of cleavage, resulted in an increased inactivation rate, and alterations to thermodynamic parameters. Lowering the pH had a similar effect on rate, but also lowered the energy barrier for viral inactivation, hinting at a catalytic process. A 3-state mathematical model of HA conformational change was able to recapitulate experimental findings at both low and neutral pH, suggesting the transitions are key to the underlying mechanism. Although the precise point of failure remains unclear, potential causes include insertion of the HA fusion peptide into an inappropriate target membrane. This work presents areas for further investigation and a way to translate these methods and approach to identify inactivation mechanisms in other pathogens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand the mechanism of viral inactivation due to heat, particularly in the context of influenza A virus (IAV), which has historically caused significant morbidity and mortality. The background highlights the importance of understanding how viruses maintain viability in the face of environmental challenges.
Design and population
The study focused on thermostability in bulk solution, examining the inactivation of IAV under varying temperature conditions. It was conducted as part of a dissertation at the University of Edinburgh, published on 2026-11-04.
Core methods
The study employed thermodynamic analysis and a 3-state mathematical model of HA conformational change to investigate the inactivation mechanisms of IAV. It also examined modifications to HA, such as amino-acid substitution or prevention of cleavage, to assess their impact on inactivation rates.
Main results
The study found that IAV infectivity demonstrated first-order exponential decay below 50 °C, indicating a non-cooperative inactivation mechanism. Arrhenius behaviour between two linear regimes (above and below 40 °C) suggests different dominant inactivation pathways separated by temperature.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the inactivation of IAV at 40-50 °C is consistent with protein unfolding, particularly implicating the viral haemagglutinin (HA). This provides novel insights into the temperature-dependent stability of IAV and its implications for disinfection methods.
Scientific and public-health significance
Understanding the mechanism of viral inactivation due to heat can improve the efficacy of disinfection protocols and have broader applications, such as enhancing vaccine stability.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, which may limit the depth of analysis. However, the findings are supported by thermodynamic analysis and mathematical modeling, providing a moderate level of confidence in the conclusions.

哥伦比亚哥伦巴利维亚城市人口的血液寄生虫、肠寄生虫和重要人畜共患病毒诊断

Diagnóstico de hemoparásitos, enteroparásitos y virus de importancia zoonótica en poblaciones urbanas de Columba livia en Colombia
作者： Nubia Estela Matta, Andrés Aponte‐Gutiérrez, Lina Marcela Pérez Arenas, Felipe Gaitán-Albarracín, Brayan Andrés Gamboa‐Suárez, J. Berdugo, Lady Johana Correa-Higuera, Angie Daniela González
摘要中文翻译家鸽(Columba livia)是多种传染源的 储存宿主,对公共健康构成风险。在哥伦比亚,这种引进物种在城市地区很常见,并进行了多项微生物学研究。然而,其在西尼罗河病毒 (WNV) 和 禽流感 (AI) 传播中的作用仍不清楚。本研究旨在评估在波哥大、阿劳卡和阿瓜苏尔捕获的野鸽中肠道寄生虫、血液寄生虫的存在以及西尼罗河病毒和禽流感的检测。从 48 名个体身上采集了粪便样本、血液样本、口腔和泄殖腔拭子。寄生虫学分析包括胃肠道寄生虫的新鲜检查和血液寄生虫的吉姆萨染色。RT-qPCR 用于使用 TaqMan 和 SYBR-Green 检测病毒。结果表明,在阿劳卡和波哥大存在哥伦比亚血变形虫以及几种体内寄生虫,包括艾美球虫和芽囊原虫等。我们讨论潜在人畜共患病毒的分子检测结果。像这样的同人物种中持续活跃的 传播 对于在城市环境中制定预防和控制策略至关重要,在城市环境中,人类和鸽子之间的频繁接触可能会促进微生物的 传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估在波哥大、阿劳卡和阿瓜苏尔捕获的野鸽中肠道寄生虫、血液寄生虫的存在以及西尼罗河病毒 (WNV) 和 禽流感 (AI) 的检测。家鸽(Columba livia)是多种传染源的 储存宿主,对公共健康构成风险。
设计与对象
该研究于 2023 年至 2024 年间在哥伦比亚城市地区进行,特别是在波哥大、阿劳卡和阿瓜苏尔。从 48 人身上采集了粪便样本、血液样本、口腔和泄殖腔拭子。
核心方法
寄生虫学分析包括胃肠道寄生虫的新鲜检查和血液寄生虫的吉姆萨染色。RT-qPCR 用于使用 TaqMan 和 SYBR-Green 检测病毒。
主要结果
结果表明,在阿劳卡和波哥大存在哥伦比亚血变形虫以及几种体内寄生虫,包括艾美球虫和芽囊原虫等。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了天鸽在城市环境中人畜共患病原体(包括 禽流感)循环中的潜在作用。
科研与公卫意义
该研究为城市环境中的 流行病学 和 禽流感 提供了重要见解,强调需要有针对性的 监测 和控制措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单一地点和小样本量,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

Diagnóstico de hemoparásitos, enteroparásitos y virus de importancia zoonótica en poblaciones urbanas de Columba livia en Colombia

Authors: Nubia Estela Matta, Andrés Aponte‐Gutiérrez, Lina Marcela Pérez Arenas, Felipe Gaitán-Albarracín, Brayan Andrés Gamboa‐Suárez, J. Berdugo, Lady Johana Correa-Higuera, Angie Daniela González
Original abstract (English)
Domestic pigeons (Columba livia) are reservoirs of several infectious agents and represent a risk to public health. In Colombia, this introduced species is common in urban areas, where several microbiological studies have been conducted. However, its role in circulating West Nile virus (WNV) and avian influenza (AI) remains unknown. This study aimed to evaluate the presence of enteroparasites, haemoparasites, and the detection of WNV and AI in feral pigeons captured in Bogotá, Arauca and Aguazul. Faecal samples, blood samples, oral and cloacal swabs were collected from 48 individuals. Parasitological analyses included fresh examination for gastrointestinal parasites and Giemsa staining for haemoparasites. RT-qPCR was used to detect the virus using TaqMan and SYBR-Green. The results indicate the presence of Haemoproteus columbae, together with the presence of several endoparasites, including Eimeria and Blastocystis, among others, in Arauca and Bogota. We discuss the results of molecular testing for potentially zoonotic viruses. Continued active surveillance in synanthropic species such as this one is essential to establish prevention and control strategies in urban contexts, where frequent contact between humans and pigeons may facilitate the transmission of microorganisms.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the presence of enteroparasites, haemoparasites, and the detection of West Nile virus (WNV) and avian influenza (AI) in feral pigeons captured in Bogotá, Arauca and Aguazul. Domestic pigeons (Columba livia) are reservoirs of several infectious agents and represent a risk to public health.
Design and population
The study was conducted in urban areas of Colombia, specifically in Bogotá, Arauca and Aguazul, between 2023 and 2024. Faecal samples, blood samples, oral and cloacal swabs were collected from 48 individuals.
Core methods
Parasitological analyses included fresh examination for gastrointestinal parasites and Giemsa staining for haemoparasites. RT-qPCR was used to detect the virus using TaqMan and SYBR-Green.
Main results
The results indicate the presence of Haemoproteus columbae, together with the presence of several endoparasites, including Eimeria and Blastocystis, among others, in Arauca and Bogota.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the potential role of Columba livia in the circulation of zoonotic pathogens, including avian influenza, in urban environments.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides important insights into the epidemiology of avian influenza in urban settings, emphasizing the need for targeted surveillance and control measures.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single location and a small sample size, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

打破通道障碍:同荧光、不同Tm探针策略可在20分钟内在六通道PCR平台上实现七靶标呼吸道病毒检测。

Breaking the channel barrier: same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy enables seven-target respiratory virus detection on a six-channel PCR platform within 20 minutes.
作者： Yang Li, Yi-Nuo Zhao, Xiang Li, Ting Sun, Lin-Wei Zhu, Miao-Jin Zhu, Li-Guo Liang, Hang-Ping Yao
摘要中文翻译背景 具有重叠症状的呼吸道病原体(包括 SARS-CoV-2、甲型/乙型流感、H5/H7/H10 禽流感 和 RSV)共同循环和共同感染,需要在护理点进行快速多重筛查。传统多重实时PCR受硬件通道容量(≤6)的限制,难以在单管中检测​​7个靶标并区分甲型和乙型流感。这项工作在不牺牲速度或灵敏度的情况下解决了高阶多重和有限荧光通道之间的矛盾。结果我们开发了一个六通道超快速 PCR 平台,使用相同荧光、不同 Tm 探针策略,可在 20 分钟内检测单管中的七种呼吸道病毒。甲型流感和乙型流感共享 FAM 通道,但会产生不同的熔解峰(~12 °C 差异,84.8 °C 与 72.3 °C),从而可以通过熔解曲线分析进行准确分型。引物、探针和酶以冻干珠的形式预先嵌入密封盒中,以实现全自动样品到结果操作。该检测在 5 个拷贝/测试中对所有目标实现了 100% 检测,在 5-105 个拷贝/测试中实现了线性 (R2 > 0.95),效率为 90-110%,并且与 39 种非目标病原体没有交叉反应。墨盒在 25 °C 下可保持稳定 12 个月。对 271 个临床样本的验证显示与测序验证的单重 qPCR 100% 一致。意义和新颖性 这项工作首次将基于熔解曲线的同通道多重技术与冻干柱技术在超快速 PCR 平台上集成。通过克服通道限制,同时保持速度和灵敏度,该系统提供了一种实用的、可现场部署的筛查工具,用于在资源有限的环境中进行分散的呼吸道病原体检测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决使用有限数量的 PCR 通道同时保持速度和灵敏度来检测具有重叠症状的多种呼吸道病原体(包括 禽流感 A 病毒)的挑战。
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种六通道超快速 PCR 平台,能够在 20 分钟内在单管中检测​​包括 禽流感 A 病毒在内的 7 种呼吸道病毒。它使用 271 个临床样本验证了该平台。
核心方法
该平台采用相同荧光、不同Tm探针策略,将引物、探针和酶预先嵌入密封盒中,以实现全自动样品到结果操作。该测定的检测灵敏度、线性、效率和交叉反应性均经过验证。
主要结果
该检测在 5 个拷贝/测试中对所有目标实现了 100% 检测,在 5-105 个拷贝/测试中实现了线性 (R2 > 0.95),效率为 90-110%,并且与 39 种非目标病原体没有交叉反应。
解释与创新
该研究引入了一种新颖的方法,该方法将基于熔解曲线的同通道多重检测与冻干盒技术相结合,能够在六通道 PCR 平台上检测七种呼吸道病毒,而不会影响速度或灵敏度。
科研与公卫意义
该系统提供了一种实用的、可现场部署的筛查工具,用于分散式呼吸道病原体检测,特别适用于资源有限的环境中,用于识别 禽流感 A 病毒和其他共同传播的病原体。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于摘要证据,研究结果基于 271 个临床样本的验证。由于缺乏详细的统计分析或长期现场性能数据,证据强度中等。

Breaking the channel barrier: same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy enables seven-target respiratory virus detection on a six-channel PCR platform within 20 minutes.

Authors: Yang Li, Yi-Nuo Zhao, Xiang Li, Ting Sun, Lin-Wei Zhu, Miao-Jin Zhu, Li-Guo Liang, Hang-Ping Yao
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND Respiratory pathogens with overlapping symptoms-including SARS-CoV-2, influenza A/B, H5/H7/H10 avian influenza, and RSV-co-circulate and co-infect, demanding rapid multiplex screening at points of care. Conventional multiplex real-time PCR is constrained by hardware channel capacity (≤6), making it difficult to detect seven targets while distinguishing influenza A and B in a single tube. This work addresses the contradiction between high-order multiplexing and limited fluorescence channels without sacrificing speed or sensitivity. RESULTS We developed a six-channel ultra-fast PCR platform detecting seven respiratory viruses in a single tube within 20 min, using a same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy. Influenza A and B share the FAM channel yet generate distinct melting peaks (∼12 °C difference, 84.8 °C vs. 72.3 °C), enabling accurate typing via melting curve analysis. Primers, probes, and enzymes are pre-embedded as lyophilized beads in sealed cartridges for fully automated sample-to-result operation. The assay achieved 100% detection at 5 copies/test for all targets, linearity (R2 > 0.95) across 5-105 copies/test, 90-110% efficiencies, and no cross-reactivity against 39 non-target pathogens. Cartridges remained stable for 12 months at 25 °C. Validation on 271 clinical specimens showed 100% agreement with sequencing-verified single-plex qPCR. SIGNIFICANCE AND NOVELTY This work provides the first integration of melting-curve-based same-channel multiplexing with lyophilized cartridge technology on an ultra-fast PCR platform. By overcoming channel limitations while maintaining speed and sensitivity, the system offers a practical, field-deployable screening tool for decentralized respiratory pathogen detection in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of detecting multiple respiratory pathogens, including avian influenza A viruses, with overlapping symptoms, using a limited number of PCR channels while maintaining speed and sensitivity.
Design and population
The study developed a six-channel ultra-fast PCR platform capable of detecting seven respiratory viruses, including avian influenza A viruses, in a single tube within 20 minutes. It validated the platform using 271 clinical specimens.
Core methods
The platform uses a same-fluorescence, different-Tm probe strategy, with primers, probes, and enzymes pre-embedded in sealed cartridges for fully automated sample-to-result operation. The assay was validated for detection sensitivity, linearity, efficiency, and cross-reactivity.
Main results
The assay achieved 100% detection at 5 copies/test for all targets, linearity (R2 > 0.95) across 5-105 copies/test, 90-110% efficiencies, and no cross-reactivity against 39 non-target pathogens.
Interpretation and novelty
The study introduced a novel approach that integrates melting-curve-based same-channel multiplexing with lyophilized cartridge technology, enabling the detection of seven respiratory viruses on a six-channel PCR platform without compromising speed or sensitivity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The system provides a practical, field-deployable screening tool for decentralized respiratory pathogen detection, particularly useful in resource-limited settings for identifying avian influenza A viruses and other co-circulating pathogens.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the findings are based on validation with 271 clinical specimens. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of detailed statistical analysis or long-term field performance data.

H5N1 禽流感 威胁:欧洲对研究差距和准备状态的看法

The H5N1 avian influenza threat: a European Perspective on Research Gaps and State of Preparedness
作者： Lone Simonsen, Tyra Grove Krause, Anders Gorm Pedersen, Lars E Larsen, Marlies Jilles Francine Goedknegt, Ramona Trebbien, Olav Hungnes, Preben Aavitsland, Otto Helve, Richard J. Webby
摘要中文翻译自 2020 年以来,H5N1 流感分支 2.3.4.4b 通过候鸟从东南亚传播。这种扩张将频繁的 跨物种溢出 传播到野生和家养哺乳动物(包括美国的牛)中。虽然人类不直接接触受感染动物的风险较低,但 进化 的广泛存在和机会构成了大流行威胁。如果出现持续的人际传播 传播 (H2H),则采取公共卫生应对措施的时间有限。最初,需要非药物干预 (NPI),直到 疫苗 可用。自 1997 年以来 H5N1 病毒的经验表明,感染主要见于 1968 年之后出生的人,这表明 60 岁以下的人可能面临特别的风险。必须密切监测野生动物和人类动物界面中的病毒多样性和 进化,必须使用集成的 One Health 监测 和基于 基因组 的病毒选择来进一步表征,例如 H5N1 与人类受体的受体亲和力。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 禽流感 A 病毒,特别是 H5N1,并检查了 2020 年以来它们通过候鸟的传播、跨物种溢出 在哺乳动物中的传播,以及它们造成的大流行威胁。核心问题是人传人 传播 的风险以及对潜在 暴发 的准备。
证据基础与方法
该综述对 H5N1 流感分支 2.3.4.4b 进行了全面分析,强调了它自 2020 年以来通过候鸟从东南亚传播的情况。它还指出该病毒的传播导致频繁的 跨物种溢出 进入野生和家养哺乳动物,包括美国的牛。该审查包括对病毒存在和 进化 的一般观察,并强调需要对基于 One Health 监测 和 基因组 的病毒性状进行综合表征。
共识与结论
虽然人类不直接接触受感染动物的风险较低,但 H5N1 病毒 进化 的广泛存在和机会构成了重大的大流行威胁。它还强调了监测野生动物和人与动物界面的病毒多样性和 进化 以检测传播性的潜在变化的重要性。
争议与缺口
审查指出,如果出现持续的人际 传播 (H2H),采取有效的公共卫生应对措施的时间将有限。它还发现了对人类感染的具体风险因素的理解上的差距,这表明根据 H5N1 病毒的历史经验,1968 年之后出生的人可能面临特定的风险。
科研与实践启示
该审查强调需要使用综合 One Health 进化 密切监测野生动物和人与动物界面的病毒多样性和 进化。它还强调了基于 基因组 的病毒选择对于进一步表征的重要性,例如受体与人类受体的亲和力,以更好地了解它们的适应潜力和 传播。

The H5N1 avian influenza threat: a European Perspective on Research Gaps and State of Preparedness

Authors: Lone Simonsen, Tyra Grove Krause, Anders Gorm Pedersen, Lars E Larsen, Marlies Jilles Francine Goedknegt, Ramona Trebbien, Olav Hungnes, Preben Aavitsland, Otto Helve, Richard J. Webby
Original abstract (English)
H5N1 influenza clade 2.3.4.4b has spread from South East Asia through migratory birds since 2020. The expansion seeded frequent spillovers into wild and domestic mammals including cattle in the United States. While the risk for humans not in direct contact with infected animals is low, the widespread presence and opportunities for evolution constitutes a pandemic threat. If sustained human-to-human transmission (H2H) would emerge, there is limited time to mount a public health response. Initially, non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) are needed until vaccines are available. Experience with H5N1 viruses since 1997 suggests that infections are seen mostly in people born after 1968, indicating that people under sixty may be at particular risk. Closely monitoring viral diversity and evolution in wild life, and at the human animal interface, using integrated One Health surveillance and genome-based selection of viruses for further characterization for instance receptor affinity of H5N1 to human receptors is mandatory.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on avian influenza A viruses, particularly H5N1, and examines their spread through migratory birds since 2020, their spillover into mammals, and the pandemic threat they pose. The central question is the risk of human-to-human transmission and the preparedness for potential outbreaks.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comprehensive analysis of H5N1 influenza clade 2.3.4.4b, highlighting its spread from South East Asia through migratory birds since 2020. It also notes the expansion of the virus leading to frequent spillovers into wild and domestic mammals, including cattle in the United States. The review includes general observations about the virus's presence and evolution, and it emphasizes the need for integrated One Health surveillance and genome-based characterization of viral traits.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that while the risk for humans not in direct contact with infected animals is low, the widespread presence and opportunities for evolution of H5N1 viruses constitute a significant pandemic threat. It also highlights the importance of monitoring viral diversity and evolution in wildlife and at the human-animal interface to detect potential changes in transmissibility.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that if sustained human-to-human transmission (H2H) were to emerge, there would be limited time to mount an effective public health response. It also identifies a gap in understanding the specific risk factors for human infections, suggesting that people born after 1968 may be at particular risk based on historical experience with H5N1 viruses.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need for closely monitoring viral diversity and evolution in wildlife and at the human-animal interface using integrated One Health surveillance. It also highlights the importance of genome-based selection of viruses for further characterization, such as receptor affinity to human receptors, to better understand their potential for adaptation and transmission.

回复:全球 禽流感 研究中普遍存在测序错误的证据。

Reply to: Evidence of widespread sequencing errors in global avian influenza study.
作者： Dhammika Leshan Wannigama, Mohan Amarasiri, Phatthranit Phattharapornjaroen, Cameron Hurst, Charin Modchang, John Jefferson V. Besa, Kazuhiko Miyanaga, Long-Zhu Cui, Stefan Fernandez, Angkana T. Huang
摘要中文翻译主要问题是由于向 GISAID 提交序列过程中出现错误而引起的。根据 GISAID 批量提交的建议,我们使用命令行软件“FluCLI”上传数据。在重新格式化文件以供提交期间,最初为构建系统发育树而准备​​的分析文件(包含我们的研究序列和参考序列)被无意中上传。此外,样本标识符的重新格式化不正确,导致研究序列和参考序列的重复和混合。重要的是,在我们最初的分析中,我们没有发现此类异常现象。对于错误序列造成的混乱,我们深表歉意。这些序列已在 GISAID 和 GenBank 中进行了纠正,其登录号与原稿中提供的相同登录号(GISAID:EPI_ISL_19613337 至 EPI_ISL_19613573/EPI_ISL_19812857-EPI_ISL_19812874;GenBank:PV422873-PV423054,PV422241-PV422430 和 PV440337-PV440372,现在版本 2)。这项工作的其他数据可通过以下链接获取:https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/KJDNGF(根据每个国家的数据保护法,这些数据严格按照合作协议的有效请求提供。它们不得用于建模、预测或以其他方式推断健康或商业应用。这些数据不允许用于生成人工智能建模、仪表板开发或与边境相关的健康预测系统。

根据 GISAID 批量提交的建议,我们使用命令行软件“FluCLI”上传数据。

Reply to: Evidence of widespread sequencing errors in global avian influenza study.

Authors: Dhammika Leshan Wannigama, Mohan Amarasiri, Phatthranit Phattharapornjaroen, Cameron Hurst, Charin Modchang, John Jefferson V. Besa, Kazuhiko Miyanaga, Long-Zhu Cui, Stefan Fernandez, Angkana T. Huang
Original abstract (English)
The primary issue arose due to an error during the sequence submission process to GISAID. Following GISAID’s advice for bulk submissions, we used the command-line software “FluCLI” to upload our data. During reformatting of files for submission, an analysis file originally prepared for phylogenetic tree construction and containing both our study sequences and reference sequences was inadvertently uploaded. In addition, sample identifiers were reformatted incorrectly, which led to duplication and mixing of study and reference sequences. Importantly, in our original analyses, we did not find such anomalies. We would like to apologize for the confusion caused by the erroneous sequences. The sequences have been corrected both in GISAID and in GenBank, under the same accession numbers provided in the original manuscript (GISAID: EPI_ISL_19613337 to EPI_ISL_19613573/ EPI_ISL_19812857-EPI_ISL_19812874; GenBank: PV422873-PV423054, PV422241-PV422430, and PV440337-PV440372, now version 2). Additional data from this work are available from the following link: https://doi.org/10.7910/DVN/KJDNGF (In accordance with each country’s data protection laws, these data are provided strictly upon a valid request according to collaborative agreements. They must not be used to model, predict, or otherwise infer health or commercial applications. The data are not permitted for use in generative AI modeling, dashboard development, or border-related health prediction systems.

人类甲型流感 (H5N1) 感染的 抗病毒药物 治疗:治疗 时机、临床结果、病毒学结果和 抗病毒药物 耐药性:系统评价

Antiviral Treatment of Human Influenza A(H5N1) Infection: Treatment Timing, Clinical Outcomes, Virological Outcomes, and Antiviral Resistance: A Systematic Review
作者： Haaly Al Mu’aafy Ruslan Musanip, Juniastuti Juniastuti, Kaiser Gerald Handoyo, Rizal Rachbini Mahlevi
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注人类甲型流感(H5N1)感染的 抗病毒药物 抗病毒药物,检查 治疗 时机、临床结果、病毒学结果和 抗病毒药物 耐药性。本主题仅限于 禽流感 A 病毒、禽源人畜共患感染和合格的禽亚型,特别强调 H5N1。
证据基础与方法
从数据库建立到 2026 年 7 月 1 日,对 PubMed、Scopus 和 ScienceDirect 进行了系统检索。使用纽卡斯尔-渥太华量表和 JBI 病例系列清单评估方法学质量。不同研究的临床和方法学特征各不相同,导致对研究结果进行叙述性综合。
共识与结论
Oseltamivir treatment was associated with a lower hazard of death compared to no documented anti-influenza antiviral treatment (adjusted hazard ratio 0.51, 95% confidence interval 0.34–0.77). Treatment within 2 days showed lower odds of death, although the overall estimate was not statistically significant (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–1.04). Among patients without respiratory failure at treatment initiation, earlier treatment was associated with lower mortality (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–0.89). Overall, oseltamivir, particularly when initiated early and before advanced respiratory failure, was associated with improved clinical outcomes.
争议与缺口
研究结果受到观察设计、异质 治疗 时间安排以及可能重叠的患者群体的限制。因果推断仍然受到混杂、异质性和重叠研究群体的限制。其他 抗病毒药物 的证据有限,并且确定了一项确认的 治疗 出现的 H274Y 神经氨酸酶抗性事件。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步的研究来阐明 治疗 时机与临床结果之间的因果关系。监测 对 抗病毒药物 的耐药性应加强,特别是对奥司他韦。为了提高未来研究结果的可靠性,需要改进研究设计、明确的人群定义和标准化的 治疗 协议。

Antiviral Treatment of Human Influenza A(H5N1) Infection: Treatment Timing, Clinical Outcomes, Virological Outcomes, and Antiviral Resistance: A Systematic Review

Authors: Haaly Al Mu’aafy Ruslan Musanip, Juniastuti Juniastuti, Kaiser Gerald Handoyo, Rizal Rachbini Mahlevi
Original abstract (English)
Human influenza A(H5N1) infection remains a clinically important zoonotic disease, but evidence on antiviral effectiveness is limited by observational designs, heterogeneous treatment timing, and potentially overlapping patient populations. This systematic review synthesized evidence on antiviral regimens, treatment timing, clinical and virological outcomes, and antiviral resistance in laboratory-confirmed human A(H5N1) infection. A systematic search of PubMed, Scopus, and ScienceDirect was conducted from database inception to 1 July 2026. Methodological quality was assessed using the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale and the JBI Case Series Checklist. The clinical and methodological characteristics varied across studies; consequently, the findings were synthesized narratively. Potential population overlap was considered when synthesizing findings across publications to avoid duplicate aggregation of patient data. Of 2,329 records identified, 22 publications were included. Oseltamivir treatment was associated with a lower hazard of death than no documented anti-influenza antiviral treatment (adjusted hazard ratio 0.51, 95% confidence interval 0.34–0.77). Treatment within 2 days showed lower odds of death, although the overall estimate was not statistically significant (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–1.04). Among patients without respiratory failure at treatment initiation, earlier treatment was associated with lower mortality (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–0.89). Evidence for other antivirals was limited, and one confirmed treatment-emergent H274Y neuraminidase resistance event was identified. Overall, oseltamivir, particularly when initiated early and before advanced respiratory failure, was associated with improved clinical outcomes, although causal inference remains limited by confounding, heterogeneity, and potentially overlapping study populations.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on antiviral treatment of human influenza A(H5N1) infection, examining treatment timing, clinical outcomes, virological outcomes, and antiviral resistance. The topic is limited to avian influenza A viruses, avian-origin zoonotic infections, and qualified avian subtypes, with a specific emphasis on H5N1.
Evidence base and method
A systematic search of PubMed, Scopus, and ScienceDirect was conducted from database inception to 1 July 2026. Methodological quality was assessed using the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale and the JBI Case Series Checklist. The clinical and methodological characteristics varied across studies, leading to a narrative synthesis of findings.
Consensus and conclusions
Oseltamivir treatment was associated with a lower hazard of death compared to no documented anti-influenza antiviral treatment (adjusted hazard ratio 0.51, 95% confidence interval 0.34–0.77). Treatment within 2 days showed lower odds of death, although the overall estimate was not statistically significant (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–1.04). Among patients without respiratory failure at treatment initiation, earlier treatment was associated with lower mortality (adjusted odds ratio 0.17, 95% confidence interval 0.03–0.89). Overall, oseltamivir, particularly when initiated early and before advanced respiratory failure, was associated with improved clinical outcomes.
Controversies and gaps
The findings are limited by observational designs, heterogeneous treatment timing, and potentially overlapping patient populations. Causal inference remains constrained by confounding, heterogeneity, and overlapping study populations. The evidence for other antivirals is limited, and one confirmed treatment-emergent H274Y neuraminidase resistance event was identified.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to clarify the causal relationship between treatment timing and clinical outcomes. Surveillance for antiviral resistance should be strengthened, particularly for oseltamivir. Improved study designs with clear population definitions and standardized treatment protocols are required to enhance the reliability of future findings.

禽流感 病毒 传播 模型的系统回顾:差距与机遇

A systematic review of avian influenza virus transmission models: Gaps and opportunities
作者： Sheng-Lai Yin, Yan-Jie Xu, Claire S. Teitelbaum, Johanna A. Harvey, John Y. Takekawa, Wayne Marcus Getz, Diann J. Prosser, Xiang-Ming Xiao
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性禽流感 病毒(HPAIV)日益威胁 野生鸟类、家禽、非人类哺乳动物和人类,但 禽流感 病毒(AIV)传播 模型的差距和机遇尚未全面综合。我们回顾了截至 2025 年发表的 182 篇文章,对维度进行了分类,包括建模方法、数据、宿主 类型、生物水平、空间尺度、时间尺度和模型目标,检查了这些维度之间的关联,并综合了作者陈述的限制和建议。统计和机械模型占主导地位。模型数据集中于人口动态和人口统计学、疾病 监测、宿主 流行病学 和能力、气象以及土地利用和土地覆盖。大多数研究集中在区域空间尺度、季节/年度和多年时间尺度、种群或多种群水平上的家禽或野生鸟类,并且是回顾性的。机器学习和决策模型不太常见。免疫、行为和生活史、群落组成、形态、病毒遗传学和病毒持续性受到的关注较少。非人类哺乳动物、个体和群落的生物水平、局部和更广泛的空间尺度、日常时间尺度和前瞻性建模也较少进行建模。作者经常报告与数据可用性、监测 抽样和报告偏差以及模型简化相关的局限性,同时通常建议提高数据质量和提高模型复杂性和真实性。未来的研究可以围绕明确定义的 传播 流程调整和集成建模方法、数据和结构,同时考虑不确定性并保持模型复杂性与经验证据相称。优先事项包括模型引导的 监测(可解释不完善的检测)、正在研究的 宿主 和 传播 流程的更广泛集成、更具前瞻性的建模以及机器学习和决策的扩展应用(在适当情况下)。跨越时空尺度和生物水平的问题可能需要通过基于主体或基于个体的模型进行多尺度和多层次集成,并辅以分层贝叶斯推理或围绕共享目标设计的链接模块。

A systematic review of avian influenza virus transmission models: Gaps and opportunities

Authors: Sheng-Lai Yin, Yan-Jie Xu, Claire S. Teitelbaum, Johanna A. Harvey, John Y. Takekawa, Wayne Marcus Getz, Diann J. Prosser, Xiang-Ming Xiao
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Highly pathogenic avian influenza virus (HPAIV) increasingly threatens wild birds, poultry, non‐human mammals and humans, yet gaps and opportunities in avian influenza virus (AIV) transmission modelling have not been comprehensively synthesized. We reviewed 182 articles published through 2025, categorized dimensions, including modelling approaches, data, host types, biological levels, spatial scales, temporal scales and model objectives, examined associations among these dimensions, and synthesized author‐stated limitations and recommendations. Statistical and mechanistic models dominated. Model data centred on population dynamics and demography, disease surveillance, host epidemiology and competence, meteorology and land use and land cover. Most studies focused on poultry or wild birds at regional spatial scales, seasonal/annual and multi‐year temporal scales, population or multi‐population levels, and were retrospective. Machine learning and decision‐making models were less common. Immunity, behaviour and life history, community composition, morphology, viral genetics and viral persistence received less attention. Non‐human mammals, individual and community biological levels, local and broader spatial scales, daily temporal scales and prospective modelling were also less frequently modelled. The authors frequently reported limitations related to data availability, surveillance sampling and reporting bias, and model simplification, while commonly recommending improved data quality and greater model complexity and realism. Future studies can align and integrate modelling approaches, data and structures around clearly defined transmission processes, while accounting for uncertainty and keeping model complexity commensurate with empirical evidence. Priorities include model‐guided surveillance that accounts for imperfect detection, broader integration of understudied hosts and transmission processes, more prospective modelling and expanded applications of machine learning and decision‐making where appropriate. Questions spanning spatiotemporal scales and biological levels may require multi‐scale and multi‐level integration through agent‐based or individual‐based models, complemented with hierarchical Bayesian inference, or linked modules designed around shared objectives.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Avian influenza A viruses, avian-origin zoonotic infections, and qualified avian subtypes. Biologically related animal, taxonomic, model, differential-diagnosis and comparative entities are supplementary-only unless material target-specific evidence is also present; only explicit lexical or unrelated hard exclusions are terminal.
Evidence base and method
We reviewed 182 articles published through 2025, categorized dimensions, including modelling approaches, data, host types, biological levels, spatial scales, temporal scales and model objectives, examined associations among these dimensions, and synthesized author‐stated limitations and recommendations.
Consensus and conclusions
Statistical and mechanistic models dominated.
Controversies and gaps
Model data centred on population dynamics and demography, disease surveillance, host epidemiology and competence, meteorology and land use and land cover. The authors frequently reported limitations related to data availability, surveillance sampling and reporting bias, and model simplification, while commonly recommending improved data quality and greater model complexity and realism.
Research and practice implications
Priorities include model‐guided surveillance that accounts for imperfect detection, broader integration of understudied hosts and transmission processes, more prospective modelling and expanded applications of machine learning and decision‐making where appropriate.

Epidemiology, co-infection and risk factors of major poultry respiratory pathogens in North Africa: a narrative review (2000-2025)

范围与问题

科研与实践启示
迫切需要加强分子 监测、基因型匹配的 疫苗接种、协调的区域监测和基于“同一个健康”的控制策略,以减轻疾病负担并减轻与 H5N1、H9N2 和多重耐药禽致病性大肠杆菌相关的人畜共患风险。

Epidemiology, co-infection and risk factors of major poultry respiratory pathogens in North Africa: a narrative review (2000-2025)

Authors: Diano Diop, Siham FELLAHI
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Poultry respiratory diseases are a major threat to food security, farmer livelihoods, and public health in North Africa. The region comprises Morocco, Algeria, Tunisia, Egypt, Libya, and Mauritania, where commercial and backyard poultry systems are highly susceptible to respiratory pathogens. Disease emergence and spread are facilitated by major migratory bird flyways, live bird markets, cross-border poultry trade, limited surveillance and diagnostic capacity in several countries. Methods A narrative review of peer-reviewed literature published between January 2000 and December 2025 was conducted using PubMed, Scopus, Web of Science, Google Scholar, and ScienceDirect. Search terms included “poultry respiratory disease,” “avian influenza,” “Newcastle disease,” “infectious bronchitis,” “avian metapneumovirus,” “Mycoplasma gallisepticum,” “co-infection,” “epidemiology,” and the names of the six North African countries. Original prevalence, surveillance, molecular, and epidemiological studies were included, while grey literature and conference abstracts were excluded. FAO and WOAH reports supplemented data for Mauritania and Libya. Of 561 records identified, 273 met the inclusion criteria. Results Newcastle disease virus (NDV) and infectious bronchitis virus (IBV) are enzootic throughout North Africa, with the highest reported prevalence in Algeria (NDV: 82.7%; IBV: 78.2%) values are estimates from individual studies rather than nationally representative prevalence estimates. Low-pathogenic avian influenza H9N2 is endemic across all six countries, while highly pathogenic H5N1 has been reported in Egypt, Libya, Algeria, and Mauritania, and H5N8 in Egypt and Algeria. Avian metapneumovirus subtype B circulates in Morocco, Algeria, and Tunisia, with seroprevalence reaching 79.9% in unvaccinated Moroccan broilers. Mycoplasma gallisepticum and Mycoplasma synoviae are widely reported. Multi-pathogen co-infections are common, increasing disease severity, mortality, and vaccine failure. Surveillance remains uneven, with Morocco and Egypt providing the strongest evidence, and substantial data gaps persist in Libya and Mauritania. Conclusions North Africa is a high-risk region for the emergence and spread of poultry respiratory pathogens. Strengthened molecular surveillance, genotype-matched vaccination, harmonized regional monitoring, and One Health-based control strategies are urgently needed to reduce disease burden and mitigate zoonotic risks associated with H5N1, H9N2, and multidrug-resistant avian pathogenic Escherichia coli .

Scope and question
Avian influenza A viruses, avian-origin zoonotic infections, and qualified avian subtypes. Biologically related animal, taxonomic, model, differential-diagnosis and comparative entities are supplementary-only unless material target-specific evidence is also present; only explicit lexical or unrelated hard exclusions are terminal.
Evidence base and method
A narrative review of peer-reviewed literature published between January 2000 and December 2025 was conducted using PubMed, Scopus, Web of Science, Google Scholar, and ScienceDirect. Search terms included “poultry respiratory disease,” “avian influenza,” “Newcastle disease,” “infectious bronchitis,” “avian metapneumovirus,” “Mycoplasma gallisepticum,” “co-infection,” “epidemiology,” and the names of the six North African countries. Original prevalence, surveillance, molecular, and epidemiological studies were included, while grey literature and conference abstracts were excluded.
Consensus and conclusions
Newcastle disease virus (NDV) and infectious bronchitis virus (IBV) are enzootic throughout North Africa, with the highest reported prevalence in Algeria (NDV: 82.7%; IBV: 78.2%) values are estimates from individual studies rather than nationally representative prevalence estimates. Low-pathogenic avian influenza H9N2 is endemic across all six countries, while highly pathogenic H5N1 has been reported in Egypt, Libya, Algeria, and Mauritania, and H5N8 in Egypt and Algeria. Mycoplasma gallisepticum and Mycoplasma synoviae are widely reported. Multi-pathogen co-infections are common, increasing disease severity, mortality, and vaccine failure.
Controversies and gaps
Surveillance remains uneven, with Morocco and Egypt providing the strongest evidence, and substantial data gaps persist in Libya and Mauritania. FAO and WOAH reports supplemented data for Mauritania and Libya.
Research and practice implications
Strengthened molecular surveillance, genotype-matched vaccination, harmonized regional monitoring, and One Health-based control strategies are urgently needed to reduce disease burden and mitigate zoonotic risks associated with H5N1, H9N2, and multidrug-resistant avian pathogenic Escherichia coli.

Journal: Journal of Psychology and Neuroscience  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-05  |  DOI: 10.47485/2693-2490.1181  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window

禽流感 的心理影响

The Psychological Impacts of Avian Influenza
作者： Steven Taylor
摘要中文翻译在制定 禽流感 (AVI) 大流行指南时,心理因素被忽视了。为了解决这一差距,我们进行了一项叙述性文献综述,以(1)研究 AVI 暴发(迄今为止主要限于牲畜)如何对人们的心理产生影响,以及(2)考虑如果出现 AVI 大流行(大量人将被感染)的影响。调查结果表明,AVI 暴发 的特点是害怕、厌恶和回避家禽产品。AVI 暴发 中观察到的许多其他心理现象在过去的流行病和其他传染性 暴发 中都有记录:责备和愤怒、与疾病相关的回避和污名化、仇外心理、阴谋论、恐慌性购买和个人囤积都是针对 AVI 暴发 的反应而出现的。如果 AVI 暴发 演变成流行病,这些心理现象可能会持续存在并加剧。从 COVID-19 中汲取的经验教训可用于为可能发生的 AVI 大流行做好准备。然而,可能需要采取额外的措施。对特定食物的恐惧、厌恶和回避可能会区分 AVI 大流行和 COVID-19。受感染食物的威胁会引发厌恶,因此与食物相关的厌恶可能是 AVI 大流行期间的一个突出的情绪反应。来自卫生当局和其他机构的公共信息有可能减轻或加剧公众对食品安全的担忧,具体取决于信息的制作方式。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了 禽流感 (AVI) 暴发 如何对人们的心理产生影响,并考虑了如果出现 AVI 大流行(大量人将被感染)的影响。
证据基础与方法
我们进行了一项叙述性文献综述,以研究 AVI 暴发 的心理影响,并考虑对潜在大流行的影响。该审查不报告具体数据库、检索日期或资格标准。
共识与结论
调查结果表明,AVI 暴发 的特点是害怕、厌恶和回避家禽产品。如果 AVI 暴发 演变成流行病,这些心理现象可能会持续存在并加剧。
争议与缺口
审查指出,在 AVI 暴发 中观察到的许多心理现象在过去的流行病和其他传染病 暴发 中都有记录。然而,证据并未报告研究结果中的具体冲突或异质性。
科研与实践启示
卫生当局发布的公共信息有可能减轻或加剧公众对食品安全的担忧,具体取决于信息的制作方式。从 COVID-19 中汲取的经验教训可用于为可能发生的 AVI 大流行做好准备,但可能需要采取其他措施。

The Psychological Impacts of Avian Influenza

Authors: Steven Taylor
Original abstract (English)
Psychological factors have been neglected in preparation guidelines for an avian influenza (AVI) pandemic. To address this gap, a narrative literature review was conducted to (1) examine how AVI outbreaks—so far largely contained to livestock—have psychologically impacted people, and (2) consider the implications if an AVI pandemic were to emerge, in which large numbers of people would be infected. Findings indicate that AVI outbreaks have been characterized by fear, disgust, and avoidance of poultry products. Many of the other psychological phenomena observed in AVI outbreaks have been documented in past pandemics and other infectious outbreaks: Blame and anger, disease-related avoidance and stigmatization, xenophobia, conspiracy theories, panic buying, and personal stockpiling have all arisen in response to AVI outbreaks. These psychological phenomena may persist and intensify if AVI outbreaks evolve into a pandemic. The lessons learned from COVID-19 can be used to prepare for a possible AVI pandemic. However, additional measures may be needed. Fear, disgust, and avoidance of specific foods would likely distinguish an AVI pandemic from COVID-19. The threat of infected food triggers disgust, and so food-related disgust would likely be a prominent emotional reaction during an AVI pandemic. Public messaging from health authorities and others has the potential to either ease or exacerbate public concerns about food safety, depending on how the messages are crafted.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines how avian influenza (AVI) outbreaks have psychologically impacted people, and considers the implications if an AVI pandemic were to emerge, in which large numbers of people would be infected.
Evidence base and method
A narrative literature review was conducted to examine psychological impacts of AVI outbreaks and consider implications for a potential pandemic. The review does not report specific databases, search dates, or eligibility criteria.
Consensus and conclusions
Findings indicate that AVI outbreaks have been characterized by fear, disgust, and avoidance of poultry products. These psychological phenomena may persist and intensify if AVI outbreaks evolve into a pandemic.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that many psychological phenomena observed in AVI outbreaks have been documented in past pandemics and other infectious outbreaks. However, the evidence does not report specific conflicts or heterogeneity in findings.
Research and practice implications
Public messaging from health authorities has the potential to either ease or exacerbate public concerns about food safety, depending on how the messages are crafted. The lessons learned from COVID-19 can be used to prepare for a possible AVI pandemic, but additional measures may be needed.
