地点与对象
印第安纳州,美国;奶牛群。
事件
美国农业部动植物检疫局 (USDA-APHIS) 报告了印第安纳州奶牛群中首例 H5N1 确诊病例。该病例通过聚合酶链反应(PCR)检测H5N1基因型B3.13确诊。
规模、影响与风险
这标志着印第安纳州首次报告牛感染高致病性禽流感病例。此次检测不会改变美国农业部的高致病性禽流感根除策略。生物安全被强调为减轻疾病传播风险的关键。一般公众的风险被认为较低,但那些因工作或娱乐而接触受感染鸟类或哺乳动物的人风险较高。
应对、状态与不确定性
APHIS 正在开展额外的农场调查、诊断测试和流行病学数据收集,以了解检测情况并防止进一步传播。此次检测不会影响商业牛奶供应的安全,因为受影响动物的牛奶正在被转移或销毁。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Indiana’s first case of H5N1 in dairy herd confirmed

News Brief
On November 3, 2026, the U.S. Department of Agriculture-Animal & Plant Health Inspection Service (USDA-APHIS) confirmed the first case of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 genotype B3.13 in a dairy cattle herd in Indiana. The detection was made through routine National Milk Testing Strategy testing, not pre-movement surveillance. This marks the first reported case of HPAI in cattle in Indiana, though dairy cattle in 21 states have been infected since March 2024. USDA-APHIS is conducting on-farm investigations, diagnostic testing, and data collection to understand the situation and prevent further spread. The detection does not alter USDA’s HPAI eradication strategy, and biosecurity measures are emphasized to mitigate disease introduction or spread. The risk to the general public is low, but individuals with job-related or recreational exposure to infected birds or mammals are at higher risk and should follow CDC guidance. Milk from affected animals is being diverted or destroyed to ensure the safety of the commercial milk supply, as pasteurization is effective at inactivating the virus. Producers are advised to report any livestock with clinical signs or unusual sick or dead wildlife to their state veterinarian.
Original source text (English)

USDA-APHIS November 3, 2026 schedule2 Min Read The U.S. Department of Agriculture-Animal & Plant Health Inspection Service’s National Veterinary Services Laboratories confirmed via polymerase chain reaction (PCR) detection of highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) H5N1 genotype B3.13 in a dairy cattle herd in Indiana. This detection was a result of routine National Milk Testing Strategy testing, not pre-movement surveillance. This marks the first reported case of HPAI in cattle in Indiana. While dairy cattle in a total of 21 states have been infected since the start of the outbreak in March 2024, APHIS has seen cases in previously unaffected states this year. APHIS is working closely with the Indiana State Board of Animal Health to conduct additional on-farm investigations, diagnostic testing and epidemiological data collection to better understand the detection and prevent further disease spread. The detection does not change USDA’s HPAI eradication strategy. Biosecurity is still key to mitigating the risk of disease introduction or spread between premises. APHIS recommends enhanced biosecurity measures for all dairy farms. Producers should immediately report any livestock with clinical signs, or any unusual sick or dead wildlife, to their state veterinarian. Related:IgG levels tracked in dairy calves fed colostrum replacer This detection does not pose a risk to consumer health or affect the safety of the commercial milk supply. The U.S. Food & Drug Administration is confident that pasteurization is effective at inactivating HPAI virus and that the commercial, pasteurized milk supply is safe. Dairies are required to send milk only from healthy animals into processing for human consumption; milk from impacted animals is being diverted from the commercial milk tank or destroyed so that it does not enter the human food supply. According to the U.S. Centers for Disease Control & Prevention, HPAI viruses circulating in birds and U.S. dairy cattle pose a low risk to the general public in the U.S. However, people who have job-related or recreational exposures to infected birds or mammals are at higher risk of infection and should take appropriate precautions outlined in CDC guidance. First U.S. Bird Flu Death of 2026 Confirmed Following Washington Resident's Exposure to Infected Dairy Cows - Discover Magazine

News Brief
The first U.S. bird flu death of 2026 has been confirmed following exposure to infected dairy cows in Washington. The report, published by Discover Magazine on November 3, 2026, highlights the case of a resident who contracted the virus after contact with the infected animals. The evidence provided does not specify the exact strain of the avian influenza virus involved, nor does it detail the broader impact or risk level of the outbreak. The confirmation of the death underscores the potential for zoonotic transmission from animals to humans, a concern that has been increasingly noted in recent years. However, the source is a syndicated summary, and the full original article body could not be extracted, limiting the depth of the information available. This case serves as a reminder of the importance of monitoring and controlling avian influenza outbreaks, particularly in regions where livestock and human populations interact closely.

B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

印第安纳州奶牛群中检测到禽流感 - InkFreeNews.com

Bird Flu Detected In Indiana Dairy Herds - InkFreeNews.com
新闻简报2026 年 11 月 4 日,印第安纳州确认牛顿县两家相连的奶牛场存在 高致病性禽流感 (H5N1),这标志着该州奶牛失去了无禽流感状态。该病毒是通过国家 监测 计划下的常规牛奶监测发现的,而不是通过牛的明显患病迹象发现的。这促使州政府官员对受影响的牛群实施行动限制,并扩大 监测 的努力。方圆10公里内的奶牛场和商业家禽养殖场也正在反复测试。联邦对州际牲畜运输的要求有所提高,并鼓励生产者参与畜群状况认证计划。官员们表示,商业加工的牛奶和妥善处理的动物产品仍然是安全的,但生奶存在潜在风险。建议宠物主人避免给猫喂食生奶或未煮熟的动物产品,因为它们容易感染严重的 H5N1 疾病。野生动物专家正在监测附近的鸟类种群,特别是迁徙水禽,这些鸟类与之前的 暴发 有关。此次检测凸显了生物安全措施和正在进行的 监测 在防止 禽流感 传播方面的重要性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
跳至内容保护民主。
地点与对象
印第安纳州,特别是牛顿县,10 公里范围内的奶牛群和家禽养殖业受到影响。
事件
通过国家 监测 计划下的常规牛奶监测,印第安纳州牛顿县的两家相连乳品厂确认了 高致病性禽流感 (H5N1)。这标志着印第安纳州奶牛失去了无禽流感状态。
规模、影响与风险
奶牛群中发现了感染,导致行动限制并扩大了 监测。联邦对州际牲畜运输的要求有所提高,受影响的畜群必须满足取消限制的测试要求。野生动物专家正在监测附近的鸟类种群,特别是与之前的 暴发 有关的迁徙水禽。生奶存在潜在风险,宠物主人应注意不要给猫喂生奶或未煮熟的动物产品。
应对、状态与不确定性
州和联邦实验室通过后续测试证实了该病毒。受影响的牛群在满足测试要求之前将受到行动限制。10 公里半径内的奶牛场和家禽养殖场正在接受定期 监测 测试。官员们强调,商业加工的牛奶和妥善处理的动物产品仍然可以安全食用。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Bird Flu Detected In Indiana Dairy Herds - InkFreeNews.com

News Brief
On November 4, 2026, Indiana confirmed the presence of highly pathogenic avian influenza (H5N1) in two connected dairy operations in Newton County, marking the state's loss of bird flu-free status for dairy cattle. The virus was detected through routine milk monitoring under a national surveillance program, rather than through visible signs of illness in the cattle. This has prompted state officials to impose movement restrictions on the affected herds and expand surveillance efforts. Dairy farms and commercial poultry operations within a 10-kilometer radius are also being tested repeatedly. Federal requirements for interstate livestock movements have increased, and producers are encouraged to participate in a herd-status certification program. Officials stated that commercially processed milk and properly handled animal products remain safe, but raw milk poses potential risks. Pet owners are advised to avoid feeding cats raw milk or uncooked animal products due to their vulnerability to severe H5N1 illness. Wildlife specialists are monitoring nearby bird populations, particularly migratory waterfowl, which have been linked to previous outbreaks. The detection highlights the importance of biosecurity measures and ongoing surveillance in preventing the spread of avian influenza.
Original source text (English)

Photo by Freek Wolsnik, Pexels. News Release NEWTON COUNTY – Indiana has lost its bird flu-free status for dairy cattle after highly pathogenic avian influenza, H5N1, was confirmed in two connected dairy operations in Newton County, prompting state officials to impose movement restrictions and expand surveillance. The infections were identified through routine milk monitoring under a national surveillance program rather than reports of visibly sick cattle. Veterinarians and disease investigators traced the detection to milk samples flagged by a processing system. Follow-up testing by state and federal laboratories confirmed the virus. Affected herds must remain under movement restrictions until they meet testing requirements, including repeated negative results. Dairy farms and commercial poultry operations within a 10-kilometer perimeter of the infected facilities also will undergo recurring surveillance testing. Indiana now joins other states with confirmed H5N1 infections in dairy cattle. Federal requirements for interstate livestock movements have increased, and state officials are encouraging producers who regularly transport lactating cows across state lines to participate in a herd-status certification program. Producers are being urged to strengthen year-round biosecurity, monitor cattle for reduced milk production, fever, dehydration and changes in behavior, and isolate animals showing symptoms while consulting veterinarians. Wildlife specialists also are monitoring nearby bird populations, particularly migratory waterfowl, which have been associated with previous outbreaks. Officials said commercially processed milk and properly handled beef, poultry and eggs remain safe for consumption. However, raw milk poses potential risks, and pet owners are being cautioned against feeding cats raw milk or uncooked animal products because of their vulnerability to severe H5N1 illness. 亚基马县卫生官员报告了第二例 禽流感 病例 - 不间断本地新闻

Yakima County health officials report second avian influenza case - Nonstop Local News
新闻简报亚基马县卫生官员报告了第二例 禽流感 病例,华盛顿农业和卫生官员证实了这一情况。该病例是在皮尔斯县的一家商业家禽设施中发现的,该设施的工人正在接受监测。该报告于2026年11月1日发布,并于2026年11月5日下午6:08更新。目前该事件正在调查中,证据中没有提供具体病例或死亡人数。地方当局正在跟踪情况,应对措施包括监测和潜在的遏制措施。该报告来自当地新闻媒体,完整的原始文章正文可供查看。

该病例是在皮尔斯县的一家商业家禽设施中发现的,并对工人进行了监测。证据中没有提供具体的病例数、死亡数或暴露数。
应对、状态与不确定性
目前情况正在调查中,卫生官员正在监测工作人员,并将最新情况报告为事件。来源为当地新闻媒体,内容为完整报道。

华盛顿州居民死于禽流感 - 新闻

Washington State Resident Dies From Bird Flu - Newser
新闻简报华盛顿州一名居民死于禽流感并发症,这是美国一年内首次出现此类死亡事件。该人本来就有健康问题,在饲养受感染牛的奶牛场接触过 禽流感。县卫生官员证实了死亡,疾病预防控制中心将病毒株鉴定为 H5N1。该机构指出,目前尚无已知的人传人 传播,对公众的风险仍然很低。官员们正在对与死者有密切接触的个人进行接触者追踪和监测。这起死亡事件增加了 禽流感 暴发 的更广泛背景,其中 33 个奶牛群的 H5N1 检测呈阳性,以及 3 例人类感染与乳制品接触有关。CDC 分析了该病毒的基因序列,发现没有任何 突变 会增强其传播或严重性。县官员没有透露有关死者的更多细节。
查看新闻五要素
时间
事件日期为2026年11月星期日。发布日期为2026年11月3日。证据中未指定报告的时间范围。
地点与对象
地点是华盛顿州亚基马县。受影响的人群是亚基马县的居民,他之前有健康问题,并在饲养受感染牛的奶牛场接触过 禽流感。
事件
据美联社报道并经疾病预防控制中心证实,一名华盛顿州居民死于禽流感并发症。死亡归因于 禽流感,特别是 H5N1,在接触受感染的牛后。
规模、影响与风险
这是美国一年内首次因禽流感死亡。疾病预防控制中心表示,没有已知的人与人之间的传播,对公众的风险很低。三起人类感染病例与一家奶牛场的接触有关,33 个奶牛群的 H5N1 病毒检测结果呈阳性。
应对、状态与不确定性
县官员正在与州卫生部门合作,以识别和监测与感染者有过密切接触的人。疾病预防控制中心分析了病毒的基因序列,没有发现任何会增加传播性或严重性的变化。调查仍在进行中,没有公布有关死者的更多细节。
B routine priorityPublic-health news

Washington State Resident Dies From Bird Flu - Newser

News Brief
A Washington State resident has died from complications of bird flu, marking the first such death in the US in a year. The individual, who had pre-existing health conditions, was exposed to avian influenza at a dairy with infected cattle. County health officials confirmed the death, and the CDC identified the strain as H5N1. The agency noted that there is no known person-to-person transmission, and the risk to the general public remains low. Officials are conducting contact tracing and monitoring individuals who had close contact with the deceased. This death adds to a broader context of avian influenza outbreaks, with 33 dairy herds testing positive for H5N1 and three human infections linked to exposure at a dairy. The CDC has analyzed the virus's genetic sequence and found no mutations that would enhance its spread or severity. No further details about the deceased were released by county officials.
Original source text (English)

Coli Tied to Pumpkin Patch Selma Blair on Flight Incident: 'I Am So Sorry' Tailors and Cobblers Are Loving the Ozempic Boom Those Pot Gummies May Not Help You Sleep Like You Think Judge Rejects Medicaid Rule Defining 'Medically Frail' Oscar-Nominated Actor Dies in Assisted Suicide at 95 Health | bird flu Washington State Resident Dies From Bird Flu Person dies from avian influenza following exposure to infected cattle By Newser Editors and Wire Services Posted Nov 3, 2026 1:30 AM CST Copied This colorized electron microscope image released by the National Institute of Allergy and Infectious Diseases on March 26, 2024, shows avian influenza A H5N1 virus particles (yellow), grown in Madin-Darby Canine Kidney (MDCK) epithelial cells (blue). (CDC/NIAID via AP, File) A person in Washington state has died from complications of the bird flu, the first such US death in a year, the AP reports. The Yakima County resident, who had previous health conditions, was exposed to avian influenza at a dairy with infected cattle and had been hospitalized. The person died Sunday, county health officials said. Two other US deaths have been tied to bird flu since it became a national public health concern in 2024. The first, announced in January 2025, was an elderly person in Louisiana who had a version of bird flu known as H5N1. The other, in Washington state, died in November 2025 from a rarer version called H5N5. Yakima County officials did not release any additional details about the person who recently died. The Centers for Disease Control and Prevention said the person was infected with H5N1. County officials have reported that 33 dairy herds recently have tested positive for H5N1. They say three human infections have been linked to exposure at a dairy, and that the Yakima Health District is working with the state health department to find people who were in close contact with those people and monitor them for symptoms. The agency analyzed the genetic sequence of the virus from the person who died in Washington and found no changes that would make the virus spread more easily or cause more severe illness, a CDC spokesperson said Monday. There is no known person-to-person spread and the risk to the general public is low, the agency says. Read These Next Witnesses to Indiana execution describe an unusual scene. Yakima County resident dies from complications of bird flu after dairy exposure - KOMO
新闻简报据亚基马卫生区称,一名亚基马县居民在接触受感染的奶牛后死于 禽流感 并发症。2026 年 11 月 1 日发布的新闻稿报道了这起死亡事件,这是该县第三例确诊的 禽流感 人间病例。该人有潜在的健康问题,出于尊重家庭隐私,卫生区没有透露他们的姓名、性别或年龄。官员强调,尽管乳制品工人和长期接触受感染动物的人面临更高的风险,但公众感染 禽流感 的风险仍然很低。卫生区正在监测密切接触者的症状,并与公共卫生和农业官员进行协调。报告中没有提供有关病毒亚型或 传播 传播途径的具体细节。
查看新闻五要素
时间
亚基马卫生区于 2026 年 11 月 1 日星期日发布新闻稿,称该居民“今天”死于 禽流感 并发症。活动日期为2026年11月1日。

一名亚基马县居民在接触受感染牛的奶牛场后死于 禽流感 并发症。亚基马卫生区将此报告为确诊病例。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

Yakima County resident dies from complications of bird flu after dairy exposure - KOMO

News Brief
A Yakima County resident has died from complications of avian influenza after exposure to infected dairy cattle, according to the Yakima Health District. The death was reported in a news release issued on November 1, 2026, marking the third confirmed human case of avian influenza in the county. The individual had underlying health conditions, and the health district has not disclosed their name, gender, or age out of respect for the family's privacy. Officials emphasize that the risk of avian influenza infection to the general public remains low, though dairy workers and those with prolonged contact with infected animals are at higher risk. The health district is monitoring close contacts for symptoms and coordinating with public health and agriculture officials. No specific details about the virus subtype or transmission route were provided in the report.

Why is the risk of avian influenza infection low for the general public? topics: Bird flu Avian influenza Yakima County Dairy cattle Human case Public health Governor Ferguson Health district YAKIMA COUNTY, Wash. — A Yakima County resident who was hospitalized with a severe illness after contracting avian influenza following exposure at a dairy with infected cattle has died, the Yakima Health District said. In a news release issued on Sunday, the health district said the person died “today” from complications of avian influenza, also known as bird flu. The health district said the victim had underlying health conditions. “Out of respect for the family’s privacy,” the district said it is not releasing the person’s name, gender or age. Health officials said the risk of avian influenza infection to the general public remains low, but noted that people at greater risk include dairy workers and others who have close or prolonged contact with infected animals or environments contaminated with the virus. The health district said transmission of avian influenza between humans is “extremely rare” and “has never been documented in the United States.” Public health officials are continuing to monitor people who had close contact with the patient for symptoms, the district said. The situation is under investigation, and no further details on the virus subtype or transmission route are provided.

Avian influenza spread beyond a backyard coop on an Illinois farm, analysis finds

News Brief
An outbreak of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus that nearly wiped out an Illinois farmer's backyard flock also spread to a domestic cat and wild rats on the farm, an analysis of the animals by the University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign Veterinary Diagnostic Laboratory and the University of Missouri Veterinary Medical Diagnostic Laboratory found.

Location and population
Illinois farm, domestic cat, wild rats.

An outbreak of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus was found to have spread beyond a backyard coop to a domestic cat and wild rats on the farm, according to an analysis by the University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign Veterinary Diagnostic Laboratory and the University of Missouri Veterinary Medical Diagnostic Laboratory.
Scale, impact and risk
The outbreak nearly wiped out the farmer's backyard flock, with the virus spreading to a domestic cat and wild rats. The scale of impact is limited to the farm, and no explicit risk level was stated in the evidence.
Response, status and uncertainty
The spread of the virus to non-avian hosts was identified through analysis by the mentioned laboratories. The status of the outbreak is confirmed based on the findings, but no further details on response measures or containment efforts were provided.

Australia 05.11.2026 Dairy Australia’s staff costs rise 9% amid restructure Dairy Australia’s employee costs rose by 9% in the 2025-26 financial year to A$25.5 million, up from A$23.4 million a year earlier, according to the organisation’s annual report, The Weekly Times reports. Director and executive compensation totalled A$3.27 million, up from A$2.9 million. According to the report, the increase in key management personnel compensation is mostly due to termination benefits associated with the executive team restructure. In August, Dairy Australia confirmed it was reviewing its organisational structure, which was expected to result in redundancies. Staff numbers stood at 180 (169.6 full-time equivalent), down from 186 (171.6) a year earlier. B 常规重要公共卫生新闻

異常斷魂!屏東確診H5N1「撲殺4.9萬隻鵪鶉」業者心死

News article (Japanese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报宜兰屏东县一养鸡场报告禽流感H5N1 暴发,导致48,977只鹌鹑被扑杀。屏东县动物检疫站证实此事,并启动限制行动、环境消毒、周边家禽养殖场监控等一系列措施。此次扑杀给运营商造成了重大经济损失,运营商表示深感悲痛。应对措施包括持续进行的 监测 和控制措施,以防止病毒进一步传播。该事件发生于 2026 年 11 月 2 日,并于 2026 年 11 月 3 日进行报道。暴发 强调在家禽养殖中采取严格的生物安全措施的必要性,因为 H5N1 是一种 高致病性禽流感 病毒,以其对鸟类种群的严重影响和潜在的人畜共患风险而闻名。

B routine priorityPublic-health news

News article (Japanese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Japanese)
異常斷魂!屏東確診H5N1「撲殺4.9萬隻鵪鶉」業者心死
News Brief
A poultry farm in Pingtung County, Yilan, reported an outbreak of H5N1 avian influenza, leading to the culling of 48,977 quails. The incident was confirmed by the Pingtung County Animal Quarantine Station, which initiated a series of measures including movement restrictions, environmental disinfection, and monitoring of surrounding poultry farms. The culling has resulted in significant financial losses for the operator, who expressed deep sorrow. The response includes ongoing surveillance and control efforts to prevent further spread of the virus. The event occurred on November 2, 2026, and was reported on November 3, 2026. The outbreak highlights the need for strict biosecurity measures in poultry farming, as H5N1 is a highly pathogenic avian influenza virus known for its severe impact on bird populations and potential zoonotic risks.
Original source text (Japanese)

4萬8977隻鵪鶉全被撲殺,業者血本無歸,欲哭無淚。(圖/翻攝畫面) 據悉,屏東縣動物防疫所10月30日接獲鹽埔鄉1處鵪鶉場主動通報飼養鵪鶉有異常死亡情形,隨即依標準作業程序啟動防疫機制,11月1日經研究所確認感染H5N1亞型高病原禽流感病毒,隔天執行撲殺作業,現場撲殺4萬8977隻鵪鶉(種禽6500隻、產蛋鵪鶉2萬077隻、未產蛋鵪鶉2萬2400隻) 廣告 廣告 另一方面進行移動管制、對場區及周邊進行噴灑消毒作業,採集檢體送農業部獸醫研究所檢驗,並同步啟動該場半徑1公里內周圍養禽場監測採樣工作及周邊半徑3公里進行訪視輔導,訪查轄內禽場有無可疑病例,以確認周圍場家禽健康情形及有無病毒活動,並持續加強高風險養禽鄉鎮養禽場周邊環境消毒,以控制疫情散播。 農業處表示,每年10月至3月為禽流感好發季節,提醒養禽業者必須落實各項生物安全措施做好人員管制,澈底消毒人員、車輛、運輸工具及其他進出禽場的器具,並維持防鳥圍網設施正常運作,每天自主觀察場內家禽健康狀況,若發現異常症狀(如精神沉鬱、食慾不振、產蛋率下降或異常死亡等),請盡速通報動物防疫所,以利控制及防範疫情蔓延。 更多三立新聞網報導 五寶媽拚議員連任...韓國瑜交代生第6胎!「警分局長尪」反應曝光 16:00 東網電視 更多新聞短片 H5型禽流感在奶牛中引發疫情。(Getty Images圖片) 美國出現今年首例人類感染禽流感病毒死亡個案,患者在奶牛場感染H5N1型禽流感病毒,周日(1日)死於併發症。 其他熱爆話題: 死者是華盛頓州亞基馬縣居民,生前有基礎疾病,在一家有感染禽流感牛隻的奶牛場接觸到病毒。美國疾病控制和預防中心周一(2日)確認,分析死者體內的病毒基因序列後未發現任何可能導致病毒更容易傳播或引發更嚴重疾病變異。亞基馬縣近期出現3宗人類感染禽流感病例,均與一家奶牛場有關,衞生官員正追蹤感染者的密切接觸人士,並監測他們的症狀。 屏東鵪鶉場爆H5N1禽流感!縣府緊急撲殺4.8萬隻 完成清消

News article (Japanese original; verified English title unavailable)
新闻简报台湾岩头县一鸽场于2026年11月1日确诊H5N1 高致病性禽流感病毒感染。县政府立即扑杀鸽子48,977只,并在受影响场方圆3公里范围内实施监测和消毒措施。当局强调家禽养殖户需要加强生物安全措施,以防止进一步传播。

News article (Japanese original; verified English title unavailable)

Original title (Japanese)
屏東鵪鶉場爆H5N1禽流感!縣府緊急撲殺4.8萬隻 完成清消
News Brief
A pigeon farm in Yantou County, Taiwan, confirmed an H5N1 highly pathogenic avian influenza virus infection on November 1, 2026. The county government immediately culled 48,977 pigeons and implemented monitoring and disinfection measures within a 3-kilometer radius of the affected farm. Authorities emphasized the need for poultry farmers to enhance biosecurity measures to prevent further spread.

屏東縣鹽埔鄉一處鵪鶉場11月1日確診感染H5N1亞型高病原禽流感病毒,屏東縣動物防疫所隨即於11月2日執行撲殺4萬8977隻鵪鶉。(圖/屏東縣動物防疫所提供) 該鵪鶉場於10月30日主動通報出現異常死亡情形,動物防疫所依標準作業程序前往採集檢體送驗,實施移動管制與場區消毒。經農業部獸醫研究所檢驗確認感染H5N1,防疫單位隔日展開處置。 本次現場共撲殺4萬8977隻鵪鶉,內容包含種禽6500隻、產蛋鵪鶉2萬77隻及未產蛋鵪鶉2萬2400隻,防疫規模相當龐大。 廣告 廣告 為防止疫情蔓延,動物防疫所針對案場半徑1公里內的養禽場進行監測採樣,確認禽隻健康狀況;半徑3公里內的養禽場則進行訪視輔導與可疑病例訪查,同時加強高風險鄉鎮養禽場周邊環境消毒。 屏東縣政府農業處長李永文表示,每年10月至隔年3月為禽流感好發季節,養禽業者須落實生物安全措施,嚴格消毒進出人員與車輛,並維持防鳥圍網正常運作以避免野鳥接觸。 李永文強調,業者每日應自主觀察家禽健康,若發現精神沉鬱、食慾不振、產蛋率下降或異常死亡,應立即通報動物防疫所,切勿隱匿,才能及早處置控制疫情。 原文出處:屏東鵪鶉場爆H5N1禽流感!縣府緊急撲殺4.8萬隻 完成清消 更多中天新聞網報導 Audio! 😢 屏東縣鹽埔鄉鵪鶉場確診H5N1禽流感 業者主動通報...2日撲殺4.8萬多隻 2026-11-03 15:50 聯合報/ 記者劉星君/屏東即時報導 禽流感 動防所昨天執行撲殺作業。圖/屏東縣政府提供 屏東縣動物防疫所10月30日接獲鹽埔鄉1處鵪鶉場主動通報飼養鵪鶉異常死亡,隨即依標準作業程序啟動防疫機制,11月1日經研究所確認感染H5N1亞型高病原禽流感病毒,2日撲殺作業,共撲殺4萬8977隻鵪鶉,並督導業者完成場區清潔與消毒。 屏東縣動防所表示,接獲禽場通報,派人到該場移動管制、對場區及周邊噴灑消毒,採集檢體送農業部獸醫研究所檢驗,同步啟動該場半徑1公里內周圍養禽場監測採樣工作及周邊半徑3公里進行訪視輔導,訪查轄內禽場有無可疑病例,以確認周圍場家禽健康情形及有無病毒活動,並持續加強高風險養禽鄉鎮養禽場周邊環境消毒,以控制疫情散播。 動防所表示,2日撲殺,共撲殺4萬8977隻鵪鶉(種禽6500隻、產蛋鵪鶉2萬77隻、未產蛋鵪鶉2萬2400隻),並再次督導業者完成場區清潔及消毒工作,及對相關聯場進行回溯追蹤以降低疫情傳播風險。 農業處長李永文表示,每年10月至3月為禽流感好發季節,提醒養禽業者須落實各項生物安全措施做好人員管制,消毒人員、車輛、運輸工具及其他進出禽場器具,維持防鳥圍網設施運作,每日自主觀察場內家禽健康狀況,若發現異常症狀(如精神沉鬱、食慾不振、產蛋率下降或異常死亡等),請盡速通報動物防疫所,以利控制及防範疫情蔓延。 屏東縣 禽流感 登入後開始簽到 會員來簽到 您即將前往會員中心 登入簽到送 LINE 美国出现今年首例人感染禽流感病毒死亡病例,患者在奶牛场感染H5N1型禽流感病毒后死于并发症。 据美联社2日报道,死者是华盛顿州亚基马县居民,生前有基础疾病,在一家有感染禽流感牛只的奶牛场接触到禽流感病毒后住院治疗。县卫生官员说该患者1日死亡,但没有发布更多细节。 美国疾病控制和预防中心确认,这名死者感染H5N1型禽流感病毒。该机构一名发言人2日说,对死者体内的病毒基因序列进行分析,未发现任何可能导 ...

华州出现2026年首例禽流感死亡病例

News article (Chinese original; verified English title unavailable)

美国疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)的一位发言人在周一确认:“雅基马县的这起病例是全美今年首例禽流感死亡病例。”据华盛顿州农业部统计,截至9月下旬,禽流感传染已波及该州至少4个商业养殖场和67个家庭散养禽群,导致超过410万只禽鸟死亡。
